Once upon a time , there was a kind mom who loved to cook . She had a little infant who was always happy . One day , mom wanted to cook a big meal for the family . She left the kitchen door open while she went to get more food . The infant saw the open door and crawled into the kitchen . The baby saw the big pot on the stove and wanted to see what was inside . But the pot was hot and the baby could get hurt . Mom came back and saw the baby near the hot pot . She quickly picked up the baby and closed the kitchen door . The family had a yummy meal together . The mom learned to always close the door to keep her baby safe . The moral of the story is to always be careful and keep little ones safe .
Once upon a time , there was a good boy named Tim . Tim loved to play outside with his friends . One day , they went to the park to have fun . At the park , there was a big slide . Tim and his friends took turns to slide down . They laughed and had a great time . The sun was shining from the west , making the day warm and bright . After playing on the slide , Tim and his friends played tag and hide - and - seek . They were very happy . When it was time to go home , they said goodbye and went back to their houses . Tim couldn't wait to play at the park again .
Once upon a time , there was a very big and hairy lion . He lived in a big forest . One day , he was very hungry . He walked and walked until he found a restaurant . Inside the restaurant , there were many people . The lion went in and said , " I am hungry . Can I eat here ? " The people were scared of the big , hairy lion . They all ran away . The lion was sad . He roared loudly , " I just want to eat ! " Then , a kind little girl came to the lion . She said , " Don't be sad , lion . I will give you food . " She gave the lion a big plate of food . The lion was very happy and thanked the girl . They became good friends and ate together at the restaurant .
Once upon a time , there was a chubby little dog named Buddy . Buddy loved to visit his friends in the park . One sunny day , Buddy went to the park to play with his friends . While playing , Buddy saw a shiny glass on the ground . He wanted to play with the glass , but his friends said , " No , Buddy ! Glass is not a toy . " Buddy did not listen and tried to play with the glass anyway . Suddenly , the glass broke and Buddy got hurt . Buddy's friends helped him and took him to his mom . His mom cleaned his cut and gave him a hug . Buddy learned that he should always listen to his friends and not play with dangerous things like glass . From that day on , Buddy always listened to his friends and played safely in the park .
Once upon a time , there was a little boy named Tim . Tim had a glove that he loved very much . He wore the glove every day when he went outside to play . One day , Tim could not find his glove . He looked under his bed and in his toy box , but it was not there . Tim felt miserable without his glove . He asked his mom , " Mom , have you seen my glove ? " Mom shook her head and said , " No , Tim . I have not seen your glove . But let's look for it together . " So , they looked in the living room , the kitchen , and the yard . But they still could not find the glove . Finally , they found the glove in the park , on the swing where Tim had played the day before . Tim was so happy to see his glove again . He said , " I missed you , glove ! " From that day on , Tim made sure to keep his glove close so he would never lose it again .
Once , there was a girl named Sara . She wanted to design a home for her pet cat , Fluffy . Fluffy was a helpless little cat who needed a warm place to sleep . One day , Sara found a big box . She thought , " This will be perfect for Fluffy's new home ! " Sara used crayons to make the box pretty . She drew flowers , trees , and the sun on it . Inside the box , she put a soft blanket for Fluffy to sleep on . Sara called her friend Tom to come and see the new home she made for Fluffy . Tom said , " Wow , Sara ! You did a great job ! " Fluffy jumped into the box and curled up on the blanket . Fluffy was very happy in her new home . Sara and Tom smiled as they watched Fluffy sleep .
Once upon a time , there was a girl named Sue . Sue had a big staff . She used the staff to help her dress . She had a hard time dressing herself . One day , Sue saw a boy named Tim . Tim was sad . He could not dress himself too . Sue wanted to help Tim . Sue went to Tim with her staff . She said , " I can help you dress . My staff can help us . " Tim was happy . They worked together to dress . It was hard , but they did not give up . They tried and tried until they could dress . After they dressed , Sue and Tim were proud . They learned that helping each other made hard things easier . They became good friends . They always helped each other . The moral of the story is to help others when they need it .

She warned Roo , " Be careful when you hop and jump . Watch out for the big rocks ! " Roo listened to his mommy and hopped away from the big rocks . He found a nice warm spot in the sun and lay down . He felt happy and safe . Roo's mommy came over and lay down next to him . They both enjoyed the warm sun together . They were a happy kangaroo family .

The bird wanted to help the mosquito . The bird said , " I will help you find your way home . " The mosquito was happy and not frightened anymore . Together , they looked for the mosquito's home . They looked up , they looked down , and they looked all around . At last , they found the mosquito's home . The mosquito was so happy to be home . The mosquito and the bird hugged each other . They had succeeded in finding the mosquito's home .

Once upon a time , in a little street , there lived a kind girl named Lily . She liked to play with her friends and help her mom and dad . One sunny day , Lily saw a small bird trying to balance on a thin branch . The bird looked sad and scared . Lily asked the bird , " Why are you sad ? " The bird said , " I can't fly well and I'm scared to fall off the branch . " Lily wanted to help . She thought of an idea and said , " I will hold your wing and help you balance until you can fly on your own . " Lily held the bird's wing and the bird started to balance better . Soon , the bird was able to fly on its own . The bird was very happy and said , " Thank you , Lily , for being so kind and helping me . " From that day on , Lily and the bird became good friends . They played together every day . The people in the street saw how Lily helped the bird and learned that being kind to others can make everyone happy .
One day , a big round ball and a small square box went to play outside . They both liked to roll down the big hill . The big round ball could roll very easily , but the small square box had a hard time rolling . The small square box felt sad . " Hey , friend , " said the big round ball , " I can help you roll better ! " The small square box was happy to hear that . They tried rolling together , side by side . The big round ball helped the small square box roll with a little push . As they rolled down the hill , the birds stopped singing and there was silence . The big round ball and the small square box rolled faster and faster , laughing together . At the bottom of the hill , they both stopped and smiled . " Thank you for helping me roll , " said the small square box . " You're welcome , " said the big round ball , " That's what friends are for ! "
Once upon a time , there was a little dog named Spot . Spot liked to perform tricks for his friends . He could jump , roll , and even dance . Spot was very good at performing tricks . One day , Spot found a rare , shiny nail in the yard . He picked it up with his mouth and showed it to his friends . They all thought the nail was very special . Spot wanted to perform a new trick with the nail . So , Spot practiced and practiced . He learned to balance the rare nail on his nose while he danced . All of his friends clapped and cheered when they saw his new trick . Spot was very happy and proud that he could perform such a fun trick with the shiny nail .
Once upon a time , there was a little rabbit named Remy . Remy loved to rush and play in the thick green grass . He liked to hop and jump all day long . The sun was bright and warm , and Remy was very happy . One day , Remy found a big hole in the ground . He thought it was a fun new place to play . But it was a trap ! A big net came up and caught Remy . He was scared and sad . He wanted to go back to the thick green grass . Remy's friend , a small bird named Bella , saw him in the trap . She was very smart and knew how to help . Bella flew down and used her beak to open the trap . Remy was free ! Remy and Bella went back to the thick green grass to play . They were both very happy and played together all day long .

Tim and Spot played all day , and they had a great adventure .

Once upon a time , there was a lonely little fish named Zip . Zip lived in a big pond . He was the only fish with a blue scale on his tail . All the other fish had green scales . Zip felt different and wished he could find a friend . One day , Zip saw a shiny thing at the bottom of the pond . He swam down to see what it was . It was a pretty scale , just like his ! Zip was so happy . He wanted to show the other fish his new find . Zip swam fast to the other fish and showed them the shiny scale . The other fish liked the shiny scale , too . They wanted to be friends with Zip now . Zip was not lonely anymore . He had lots of friends , and they all played together in the big pond .

Tom wanted to play with the bird , so he jumped from one branch to another . The bird saw Tom and started to fly away . Tom did not want the bird to go , so he had an idea . He asked the bird to play a game . They would race to the top of the tree , and the first one to get there would win . The bird agreed , and they started to race . Tom climbed fast , but the bird was faster . In the end , the bird won the race . Tom was happy to have a new friend , and they played together every day .

One day , Tom went to the park to play . He saw a big tree with a hole in it . Tom wanted to see what was inside the hole . He removed his cap and put his hand in the hole . He felt something soft and pulled it out . It was a small , lost kitten ! Tom took the kitten home and they became best friends . The kitten liked to play with Tom's ancient cap , too . They had lots of fun together , and Tom was happy that he found a new friend by removing his cap .

Once upon a time , in a small village , there was a clumsy boy named Tom . Tom liked to play with his toy chain . One day , Tom had a question for his mom . He asked , " Mom , can I go play with my chain outside ? " His mom said , " Yes , Tom , but be careful . Don't lose your chain . " Tom was happy and ran outside to play . He swung his chain around and around . But because Tom was clumsy , the chain flew out of his hand and into a big tree . Tom was sad and had another question for his mom . " Mom , can you help me get my chain from the tree ? " His mom smiled and said , " Yes , Tom . Let's get a long stick and try to get it down . " They found a long stick and used it to get the chain back . Tom was happy again and said , " Thank you , Mom ! " From that day on , Tom tried to be less clumsy when playing with his chain .
One day , a fit dog named Max went for a jog in the park . He saw a small cat named Lily . Max said , " Hi , Lily ! Do you want to jog with me ? " Lily said , " Yes , Max ! I want to jog and be fit like you . " So , Max and Lily started to jog . They jogged up and down the hills . They jogged near the pond . They were having fun . Soon , they saw a big sign that said , " Do not follow the path into the woods . " Max and Lily did not understand big words , so they followed the path into the woods . In the woods , they got lost . Max and Lily were scared . They could not find their way home . They wished they had not followed the path into the woods . The sun went down , and it was dark . Max and Lily were sad and scared . The moral of the story is to always listen and follow the rules , even if you do not understand them . Sometimes , the rules are there to keep you safe .

As Tim climbed the pole , he got scared . He was very high up and didn't know how to get down . Tim started to weep . His tears fell down like rain . He called for his mom to help him . Tim's mom heard him weep and came running . She told him not to worry and to hold on tight . She called a big , strong man to help . The man helped Tim get down from the pole . Tim hugged his mom and said sorry for being naughty . From that day on , Tim never climbed poles again .

One sunny day , a little girl named Lily came to the shop . She saw the magic cone and asked the shopkeeper , " What is this cone for ? " The shopkeeper smiled and said , " This is a magic cone . When you say 'grow' , it will get bigger and bigger ! " Lily was very excited . She bought the magic cone and said , " Grow ! " The cone grew bigger and bigger , just like the shopkeeper said . Lily shared her big ice cream with her friends . They all laughed and enjoyed the sweet treat together . From that day on , Lily and her friends would visit the new ice cream shop every day . They loved the magic cone and the fun it brought to their little village . And they all lived happily ever after .

Once upon a time , in a big town , there was a wealthy man named Bob . He loved to bounce a ball every day . He would bounce the ball up and down , and all around his big house . One day , a new law was made in the town . The law said no one could bounce balls anymore . Bob was very sad because he loved to bounce his ball . He did not want to break the law . Bob had an idea . He went to the town and talked to the people who made the law . He told them how much he loved to bounce his ball . The people listened to Bob and changed the law . Now , everyone in the town could bounce balls again . Bob was very happy and went home to bounce his ball with a big smile on his face .

One day , Tim and his mom went to the park . They played on the swings and the slide . Tim had so much fun that he forgot about his marble . Blue was very happy that he could help his friend turn better from being dead .

He could feel its little heart beating fast . Timmy decided to take the bird home and give it some food and water . After a few days , the bird started to feel better and was no longer miserable . Timmy was happy that he could help the little bird and they became best friends .
Once upon a time , there was a little girl named Lily . She loved to play outside in the morning when the mist was still around . She would run around and laugh , but one day she fell and hurt her knee . It was uncomfortable and she started to cry . Her mom came outside and saw her crying . " What happened , Lily ? " she asked . " I fell and my knee hurts , " Lily said . Her mom kneeled down and looked at her knee . " It's just a little scrape . Let's clean it up and put a bandage on it , " she said . Lily felt better after her mom helped her . She hugged her mom and said , " Thank you for making me feel better . " They continued to play outside in the mist and had a fun day together .
Once upon a time , there was a little girl named Lily . One day , she was playing with her toys when she heard a loud noise outside . She looked out the window and saw a cop car parked outside her house . Lily went outside to see what was happening . The cop told her that there was a lost dog in the neighborhood and asked if she had seen it . Lily hadn't seen the dog , but she wanted to help . She asked the cop if she could help raise awareness about the lost dog . The cop thought that was a generous idea and gave Lily some flyers to hand out to her neighbors . Lily went door to door , telling everyone about the lost dog . Finally , someone saw the dog and called the cop . The dog was reunited with its owner , and Lily felt happy that she could help .
Once upon a time , there was a little girl named Lily . She loved to eat with her fork . She would use her strong hands to hold it and put food in her mouth . One day , Lily's mom made her favorite meal , spaghetti . Lily was so excited to eat it that she didn't listen to her mom when she said to wait for it to cool down . Lily took a big bite and burned her tongue . She cried and threw her fork on the ground . Her mom comforted her and said , " Remember , next time , wait for your food to cool down before you eat it . It's important to be patient and listen to others . " From that day on , Lily always waited for her food to cool down before eating it . She learned to be patient and listen to others . And she never threw her fork again .
Once upon a time , there was a little girl named Lily . She loved to play in the park with her friends . One day , she found a shiny rock on the ground . She picked it up and showed it to her friend , Timmy . " Wow , that's impressive ! " said Timmy . " What does impressive mean ? " asked Lily . " It means it's really cool and special , " replied Timmy . Lily wanted to keep the shiny rock , but her mom said she needed to ask permission first . So , she went to her mom and asked , " Mom , can I keep this shiny rock ? " Her mom looked at the rock and said , " Sure , but you need to put a label on it so you don't lose it . " Lily was happy that her mom permitted her to keep the rock . She put a label on it that said " Lily's special rock " and kept it in her pocket all day . She felt proud and happy to have something so impressive .
Lily and Ben were playing in the park . They liked to run , slide and swing . They saw many things : trees , flowers , birds and dogs . But they also saw something unusual . It was big and round and shiny . It was in the sky , but not very high . " What is that ? " Lily asked . " I don't know , " Ben said . " Maybe it is a balloon . " They ran to the big thing . They wanted to touch it . But when they got closer , they heard a noise . It was like a beep . And then a door opened . Out came a little green man . He had two eyes , a nose , a mouth and ears . But he also had a long tail and four arms . " Hello , " the green man said . " I am Zog . I come from another planet . Do you want to be my friends ? " Lily and Ben were scared . They did not know what to say . They looked at each other . Then they looked at Zog . He smiled . He did not look mean . He looked friendly . " Hi , " Lily said . " I am Lily . This is Ben . We are from Earth . We want to be your friends . " Zog was happy . He clapped his four hands . He said , " Yay ! I have new friends . Do you want to see my spaceship ? " Lily and Ben nodded . They were curious . They followed Zog into the big thing . They saw many buttons , lights and screens . Zog showed them how to fly the spaceship . They had fun . They flew around the park . They waved to the other children . They saw the sun , the moon and the stars . They had an unusual adventure . They learned new things . They made a new friend . They said , " Thank you , Zog . You are very nice . We hope to see you again . " Zog said , " You are welcome , Lily and Ben . You are very nice too . I hope to see you again . Goodbye . " He landed the spaceship . He gave them a hug . He went back inside . The door closed . The spaceship beeped . It flew away . It disappeared in the sky . Lily and Ben ran back to their parents . They told them what they saw . Their parents did not believe them . They thought they made up a story . But Lily and Ben knew it was true . They smiled . They had a secret . They had a special friend . They had a wonderful day .
Timmy liked to spin . He spun in his room , he spun in the yard , he spun in the park . He spun so fast that he felt dizzy and happy . He liked to spin with his toys , too . He had a big ball , a small car , a soft bear , and a shiny bottle . He spun them all on the floor and watched them go round and round . One day , he found a new toy in the kitchen . It was a chubby can with a label that had a picture of a tomato . He thought it looked like a ball , so he took it to his room and spun it on the floor . It spun very fast and made a loud noise . Timmy laughed and clapped his hands . But then , something bad happened . The can hit the wall and broke open . Red juice and seeds came out and splashed everywhere . Timmy's room was a mess . He was scared and sad . He heard his mom coming and knew he was in trouble . " Timmy , what did you do ? " his mom asked , seeing the red stains on the carpet , the bed , and the walls . She was angry and worried . " I'm sorry , mom . I was spinning the can . I thought it was a ball . I didn't know it had juice inside , " Timmy said , crying . Mom sighed and hugged him . She was not happy , but she understood . She knew Timmy liked to spin , but he had to learn to be careful . " Timmy , you can't spin everything . Some things are not toys . Some things can break and make a mess . You have to look at the label and see what's inside . Do you understand ? " she said . Timmy nodded and wiped his tears . He said he was sorry again and helped mom clean the room . He learned his lesson . He still liked to spin , but he only spun his toys . He never spun a can again .
One day , a child named Tom wanted to play with his toy car . He loved his toy car very much . It was red and shiny and fast . He took it outside and drove it on the sidewalk . He made loud noises with his mouth . Vroom , vroom ! But then , he saw another child with a bigger toy car . It was blue and shiny and faster . The other child drove it on the street . He made louder noises with his mouth . Vroom , vroom , vroom ! Tom felt sad . He wanted the bigger toy car . He thought it was better than his toy car . He thought the other child was lucky . He thought he was not . He walked up to the other child and said , " Can I play with your toy car ? Please ? " The other child looked at Tom and said , " No , you can't . This is my toy car . Go away ! " Tom felt angry . He wanted the bigger toy car . He thought it was not fair . He thought the other child was mean . He thought he was tough . He pushed the other child and grabbed his toy car . He ran away with it . He made loud noises with his mouth . Vroom , vroom , vroom ! But then , he heard a louder noise behind him . It was a big car . It was real and shiny and faster . It honked its horn . Honk , honk ! Tom was scared . He dropped the bigger toy car and ran to the sidewalk . He saw the other child crying and holding his toy car . He felt sorry . He wanted to say sorry . He thought he was wrong . He walked up to the other child and said , " I'm sorry . I was bad . Can we be friends ? " The other child looked at Tom and said , " It's okay . I'm sorry too . I was mean . Yes , we can be friends . " They smiled at each other and hugged . They played with their toy cars together . They made loud noises with their mouths . Vroom , vroom , vroom ! They were happy . They thought they were lucky . They thought they were good . They thought they were tough .

Lily and Ben were playing in the park with their toys . They saw an old man sitting on a bench . He had a long beard and a hat . He was holding a book . " Who is he ? " Lily asked Ben . " I don't know . Maybe he is a wizard , " Ben said . They walked closer to the old man . He smiled at them and said hello . " Hello , sir . What are you reading ? " Lily asked . " I'm reading a story about a brave and honest knight who saved a princess from a dragon , " the old man said . " Wow , that sounds exciting . Can you tell us the story ? " Ben asked . " Sure , I can . Come and sit with me , " the old man said . He pointed to a picture in the book . It showed the knight riding a horse and holding a sword . " This is the knight . His name is Arthur . He was very brave and honest . He always helped people who were in trouble , " the old man said . He pointed to another picture . It showed the princess in a tower and the dragon breathing fire . " This is the princess . Her name is Gwen . She was very kind and beautiful . She was trapped in the tower by the dragon . The dragon was very mean and greedy . He wanted to keep her for himself , " the old man said . He pointed to the last picture . It showed the knight fighting the dragon and the princess cheering . " This is the end of the story . Arthur fought the dragon and wounded him with his sword . The dragon ran away and never came back . Arthur freed Gwen from the tower and they hugged each other . They were very happy and grateful . They became friends and lived happily ever after , " the old man said . Lily and Ben clapped their hands and cheered . " That was a great story . Thank you , sir , " Lily said . " Yes , thank you . You are very nice and smart , " Ben said . The old man smiled and said , " You're welcome , children . You are very polite and curious . I'm glad you liked the story . It's one of my favorites . " They talked for a while and then said goodbye . Lily and Ben went back to their toys and pretended to be knights and princesses . They had a lot of fun . The old man watched them and smiled . He was happy too .
Tom and Ben were friends who liked to play music . Tom had a trumpet that he got from his dad . Ben had a drum that he made from a can and a stick . They would play songs together and have fun . One day , Tom saw a new trumpet in a shop window . It was shiny and big and had many buttons . Tom wanted the new trumpet very much . He was envious of anyone who had a better trumpet than him . He thought his old trumpet was not good enough . He asked his dad to buy him the new trumpet , but his dad said no . He said that Tom should be happy with what he had and take good care of it . He said that the new trumpet was too expensive and that Tom was not ready for it . Tom was angry and sad . He did not listen to his dad . He hid his old trumpet under his bed and did not play it anymore . He hoped that his dad would change his mind and get him the new trumpet . But his dad did not change his mind . And Tom's old trumpet did not get any love . It got dusty and dirty and rusty . It started to rot . The sound became bad and the buttons got stuck . One day , Ben came to visit Tom . He wanted to play music with him . He brought his drum and asked Tom to get his trumpet . Tom felt ashamed and did not want to show his trumpet to Ben . He said that he did not want to play music anymore . Ben was surprised and sad . He liked to play music with Tom . He asked him why he did not want to play music anymore . Tom did not answer . He just looked away . Ben looked under Tom's bed and saw the trumpet . He pulled it out and saw how rotten it was . He was shocked and sad . He asked Tom what happened to his trumpet . Tom did not answer . He just cried . Ben felt sorry for Tom . He said that he liked Tom's trumpet and that it was a good trumpet . He said that he did not care about the new trumpet in the shop window . He said that he cared about Tom and their friendship . He said that Tom should not be envious of others and that he should be grateful for what he had . He said that Tom should take good care of his trumpet and his friends . He said that Tom should play music with him again and have fun . Tom realized that Ben was right . He felt sorry for being envious and for neglecting his trumpet and his friend . He hugged Ben and said that he was sorry . He said that he liked his trumpet and that it was a good trumpet . He said that he liked Ben and that he was a good friend . He said that he wanted to play music with Ben again and have fun . He asked Ben to help him clean his trumpet and make it sound good again . Ben agreed and smiled . They cleaned the trumpet and played songs together and had fun . The moral of the story is : Be happy with what you have and take good care of it . Do not be envious of others and lose what you have . Cherish your friends and play music with them .
Sam had a big bag of marbles . He liked to play with them and make them roll and shine . He had many colors and shapes of marbles . Some were big and some were small . Some were smooth and some were bumpy . One day , he went to the park with his mom . He saw a boy who had a very special marble . It was red and blue and had a star inside . Sam thought it was the most powerful marble he had ever seen . He wanted to have it . " Hi , I'm Sam . What's your name ? " he asked the boy . " I'm Max . Do you like my marble ? " the boy said . " Yes , it's very pretty and powerful . Can I see it ? " Sam said . " Sure , but be careful . It's my favorite marble , " Max said . He gave the marble to Sam . Sam held the marble in his hand . He felt its weight and warmth . He looked at the star and wondered how it got there . He wished he could keep it . " Can I have your marble ? " he asked Max . " No , you can't . It's mine . Give it back , " Max said . " Please , I'll give you anything . I have many marbles in my bag . You can choose any one you want , " Sam said . " No , I don't want your marbles . I only want my marble . It's the best marble in the world . It's my lucky marble . It makes me happy , " Max said . Sam felt sad . He wanted to make Max happy , but he also wanted the powerful marble . He didn't know what to do . He looked at his mom . She smiled and said : " Sam , you have to give back the marble to Max . It's his marble , not yours . You can't take something that belongs to someone else . You have to respect his choice . Maybe you can be friends and play together with your marbles . Sharing is more fun than spending . " Sam nodded . He gave back the marble to Max . He said : " I'm sorry , Max . I didn't mean to be rude . Your marble is very nice , but you can keep it . I have many marbles too . Do you want to play with me ? " Max smiled . He said : " Thank you , Sam . You are very kind . I'm sorry I was angry . Your marbles are very nice too . Yes , I want to play with you . " Sam and Max became friends . They played with their marbles and had fun . They learned to share and respect each other . They both felt happy .
Sara and Tom were friends who liked to play with toys . They had many toys , but they also liked to look at the screen . The screen showed them funny pictures and sounds . Sometimes they laughed and clapped at the screen . One day , Sara and Tom found a new toy in the toy box . It was a small screen that had buttons and lights . They were curious and wanted to play with it . They took turns to press the buttons and see what happened . The screen made noises and changed colors . They were happy and excited . But then , Sara and Tom started to argue . Sara wanted to keep the screen for herself . She said , " This is my screen . I found it first . You can't have it . " Tom was angry and said , " No , this is our screen . We share it . You are not nice . " They pulled and pushed the screen , trying to take it from each other . The screen did not like this . It did not belong to Sara or Tom . It belonged to someone else . It was a gift from a kind aunt who wanted to surprise them . The screen was sad and scared . It did not want to be broken or lost . It wanted to be loved and cared for . The screen made a loud beep and flashed a message . It said , " Please stop fighting . I am not your toy . I am a gift . Please give me back to the toy box . Thank you . " Sara and Tom were surprised and ashamed . They realized they were wrong . They said sorry to each other and to the screen . They put the screen back in the toy box and hugged . They decided to play with their other toys and be cheerful and kind . The moral of the story is : Do not fight over things that do not belong to you . Be respectful and grateful for what you have . Share and play nicely with your friends .
Sara and Ben are twins . They like to play games together . One day , they play a game with cards . The cards have numbers and pictures on them . Sara and Ben take turns to pick a card and show it to each other . They have to say the number and the picture out loud . If they say it right , they get a point . If they say it wrong , they lose a point . The one with the most points at the end wins . Sara is good at the game . She knows all the numbers and pictures . She picks a card and says , " Four , dog . " She gets a point . She picks another card and says , " Six , fish . " She gets another point . She picks a third card and says , " Eight , cake . " She gets another point . She has three points . She is happy . Ben is not so good at the game . He does not know all the numbers and pictures . He picks a card and says , " Three , cat . " He is wrong . The card has a three and a hat . He loses a point . He picks another card and says , " Five , bird . " He is wrong again . The card has a five and a hand . He loses another point . He picks a third card and says , " Seven , car . " He is right . He gets a point . He has minus one point . He is sad . Sara sees that Ben is sad . She wants to make him happy . She has an idea . She says , " Ben , do you want to mail these cards to grandma ? She will like them . She will write us a letter back . We can read it together . " Ben thinks that is a good idea . He likes grandma . He likes letters . He says , " Yes , Sara , let's mail these cards to grandma . That will be fun . " Sara and Ben stop playing the game . They put the cards in an envelope . They write their names and grandma's address on it . They put a stamp on it . They go to the mailbox and mail the envelope . They hug each other . They are happy . Sara and Ben are not selfish . They share their cards with grandma . They do not care about the score . They care about each other . They are good twins .

One day , they decided to play hide and seek again while their parents were out . Sara was the seeker and Ben was the hider . He ran to the wardrobe and opened the door . Sara looked for Ben in every room , but she could not find him . She started to feel worried and sad . She went to the wardrobe and opened the door . She saw the suitcase and thought it was strange . Anna and Ben were twins who liked to play outside . They liked to dig in the sand , splash in the water , and chase the birds . But they did not like to wash their hands and faces when they came home . They said it was boring and itchy . One day , their mom told them they had a lovely surprise . She said they could go to the park and have a picnic with their friends . Anna and Ben were very happy and ran to get their hats and shoes . But mom stopped them and said , " Wait , you have to wash your hands and faces first . You are very dirty and you don't want to make your friends sick . " Anna and Ben did not want to wash . They said , " No , mom , we don't need to wash . We are not dirty and we are not sick . We have our own opinion and we think washing is silly . " Mom was not happy . She said , " I'm sorry , but you have to respect my opinion too . I am your mom and I know what is best for you . Washing is not silly , it is important and healthy . If you don't wash , you can't go to the park and have the picnic . " Anna and Ben did not like this . They wanted to go to the park and have the picnic . They looked at each other and decided to listen to mom . They said , " Okay , mom , we will wash . We are sorry for being rude . We love you and we want to make you happy . " Mom smiled and hugged them . She said , " Thank you , Anna and Ben . I love you too and I want to make you happy . I'm glad you understand that washing is good for you and for others . Now you can go to the park and have the picnic . And I have another lovely surprise for you . I made your favorite cookies for dessert . " Anna and Ben were very happy and thanked mom . They washed their hands and faces and went to the park . They had a lot of fun with their friends and enjoyed the picnic . They also shared their cookies with their friends and said they were delicious . They learned that washing was not so bad after all and that listening to mom was a good thing . They also learned that having an opinion was okay , but respecting other people's opinions was also important . They had a lovely day and went to bed with a smile .

Ben did not know what that meant . He thought it was a game . He pushed the button . Suddenly , a loud noise came from the ceiling . Water started to spray everywhere . The people in the museum got wet and scared . They ran to the door . Lily , Mom and Dad also got wet . They looked for Ben . Ben was hiding behind a big rock animal . He was sorry he pushed the button . He did not know it would make water come out . He was afraid Mom and Dad would be angry . Mom and Dad found Ben . They were not angry , but they were not happy either . They told Ben he should not push buttons that say " Do not push " . They said it was dangerous and rude . Lily gave him a hug . She said she still liked him . Mom and Dad gave him a hug too . They said they still loved him . They said they would go home and change their clothes .
