Lily liked to feel her thumb in her mouth . It made her calm and happy . She did not like to share her toys or her books . She kept them organized in her room . She did not want anyone to touch them or mess them up . One day , her cousin Max came to visit . He wanted to play with Lily . He saw her toys and books and ran to them . He picked up a doll and a car and a book . He threw them on the floor . He laughed and said , " Let's play ! " Lily was angry . She ran to Max and pushed him . She yelled , " No ! Those are mine ! Go away ! " She grabbed her toys and books and put them back in their places . She did not care that Max was crying . She did not care that he was hurt . She only cared about her things . Max's mom heard the noise and came to see what was wrong . She saw Max on the floor with a bruise on his arm . She saw Lily with her thumb in her mouth and her toys and books organized . She was sad and angry . She said to Lily , " You are a bad girl . You hurt your cousin . You do not know how to share or be kind . You are selfish and mean . You have to say sorry to Max and give him a hug . And you have to let him play with your toys and books . Or you will have no friends and no fun . " Lily did not want to say sorry or hug Max . She did not want to let him play with her things . She shook her head and said , " No ! I don't care . I like my thumb and my things . I don't need friends or fun . " Max's mom took Max and left . She said , " We will not come back until you learn how to be nice . Goodbye , Lily . " Lily was alone in her room . She felt her thumb in her mouth . She looked at her toys and books . They were organized . But they were not fun . They did not talk to her or hug her or laugh with her . They were just things . She felt sad and lonely . She wished she had a friend . She wished she had been nice to Max . She wished she had shared and played . But it was too late . She had made a bad choice . And she had to live with it . The moral of the story is : Sharing is caring . Being kind is better than being organized . Friends are more important than things .
One day , a lion was very bored . He had nothing to do in his big cage . He looked at the other animals , but they were all dull . The monkeys were sleeping , the zebras were eating , and the elephants were spraying water . The lion wanted to have some fun . He decided to roar very loud and scare the other animals . He opened his mouth and let out a big roar . RRRROOOOAAAARRR ! The monkeys woke up and jumped . The zebras ran away . The elephants stopped spraying water and looked around . The lion laughed . He thought he was very clever . But soon , the other animals got angry . They did not like the lion's roar . They wanted to be left alone . They started to make noises back at the lion . The monkeys screeched . The zebras neighed . The elephants trumpeted . The lion did not laugh anymore . He felt very annoyed . He tried to roar louder , but the other animals did not stop . They made more and more noises . The lion could not hear himself roar . He felt very sad . He wished he had not roared at the other animals . He wished he had been nice to them . He decided to say sorry to the other animals . He stopped roaring and walked to them . He looked at them with his big eyes and said , " I am sorry . I was bored . I did not mean to scare you . Can we be friends ? " The other animals looked at the lion . They saw that he was sorry . They nodded and smiled . They said , " It's okay . We forgive you . We can be friends . " The lion felt very happy . He had learned his lesson . He did not roar anymore . He played with the other animals . They had fun together . The lion was not bored anymore . He was glad .
Lily and Ben are playing with their blocks . They like to build towers and houses and cars . They also like to count their blocks and learn new numbers . " Look , Lily , I have ten blocks in my tower ! " Ben says , pointing to his tower . " One , two , three , four , five , six , seven , eight , nine , ten ! " " Wow , Ben , that's a big number ! " Lily says . " Can you show me how to make ten ? " " Sure , Lily , it's easy . You just need to add more blocks . Here , take some of mine . " Ben gives Lily some of his blocks and helps her stack them on top of each other . " Thank you , Ben . Now I have ten blocks too ! " Lily says , pointing to her tower . " One , two , three , four , five , six , seven , eight , nine , ten ! " They smile and clap their hands . They are proud of their towers and their numbers . They decide to make more towers and learn more numbers . They play until their mom calls them for lunch . They put their blocks away and run to the kitchen . They are hungry and happy . They sit on their comfortable chairs and eat their sandwiches and fruits . They tell their mom about their towers and their numbers . Their mom listens and praises them . She is proud of them too .
Tom and Lily were twins who lived in a big house with their wealthy parents . They had many toys and books and clothes , but they were not happy . They always wanted more and more , and they never shared or said thank you . One day , their parents had to go away for work , and they left Tom and Lily with their nanny , Mrs . Lee . Mrs . Lee was kind and patient , but she had rules . She told Tom and Lily to eat their breakfast , brush their teeth , and play nicely . But Tom and Lily did not listen . They threw their food on the floor , hid their toothbrushes , and fought over their toys . Mrs . Lee was very sad and tired . She decided to rest on the couch for a while . She closed her eyes and fell asleep . Tom and Lily saw this and thought it was funny . They decided to play a prank on Mrs . Lee . They went to the kitchen and opened the freezer . They took out a big ice cream cake and carried it to the living room . They put the cake on Mrs . Lee's face and laughed . But the cake was very cold and wet . It melted on Mrs . Lee's face and clothes and couch . It made a big mess . Mrs . Lee woke up and felt the cake . She was very angry and shocked . She screamed and cried . Tom and Lily were scared . They realized they had done a bad thing . They ran to their room and hid under their bed . Mrs . Lee called their parents and told them what happened . Their parents were very disappointed and sorry . They came back home and scolded Tom and Lily . They made them clean up the mess and apologize to Mrs . Lee . They also took away their toys and books and clothes for a week . They told them to learn to be grateful and respectful and kind . Tom and Lily felt very sorry and ashamed . They hugged Mrs . Lee and said they were sorry . They hugged their parents and said they loved them . They learned their lesson and promised to be better . They realized that being wealthy was not enough . They needed to be happy and good .
Sara and Ben were playing in the snow . They made a big snowman with a hat and a carrot nose . They were very happy . But then , a big boy came . He was mean and rude . He said , " This is my snowman . Go away ! " He pushed Sara and Ben and made them fall . Sara and Ben were sad and scared . They did not want to fight . They said , " Please , don't be mean . We can share the snowman . It is big enough for all of us . " But the big boy did not listen . He said , " No , it is mine . I will make it bigger and better . " He took some powder from his pocket and threw it on the snowman . He said , " This is magic powder . It will make the snowman grow and obey me . " But the powder was not magic . It was pepper . The snowman sneezed and sneezed . The hat and the carrot nose flew away . The snowman fell apart . The big boy was covered in snow and pepper . He cried and ran away . Sara and Ben felt sorry for the big boy . They said , " He was not fit to play with us . He was selfish and rude . He did not know how to share and be kind . " They decided to make a new snowman . They used the hat and the carrot nose that they found . They also found a scarf and some buttons . They made the snowman smile . They said , " This is our snowman . He is happy and friendly . He likes to share and be kind . " They played with the snowman until it was time to go home . They said , " Goodbye , snowman . We will come back tomorrow . We hope you will not melt . " The moral of the story is : Sharing and kindness are better than selfishness and rudeness .
Tom and Sam are brothers . They like to play in their room . They have a big mattress on the floor . They jump on the mattress and pretend to be mighty heroes . One day , they hear a knock on the door . It is their mom . She has a surprise for them . She says , " Boys , I have something for you . Come and see . " Tom and Sam follow their mom to the living room . They see a big box . Their mom says , " This is your new bed . It is bigger and softer than the mattress . You can sleep on it and have sweet dreams . " Tom and Sam are very happy . They hug their mom and say , " Thank you , mom . We love you . Can we try the new bed ? " Their mom says , " Of course . But first , you have to help me move the mattress . It is very heavy . We need to put it in the garage . " Tom and Sam nod . They are strong boys . They help their mom lift the mattress . They carry it to the garage . They feel proud . They gain muscles and confidence . They go back to the living room . They see their new bed . It is shiny and cozy . They climb on it and snuggle . They feel warm and safe . They say , " This is the best bed ever . We are lucky boys . We have a good mom and a good bed . " Their mom smiles . She says , " You are welcome , boys . I am glad you like it . Now , it is time for a nap . Sleep well , my mighty heroes . " Tom and Sam close their eyes . They fall asleep . They have sweet dreams .
Ben is a cheerful dog . He likes to run and play with his friends . He has a long tail that wags when he is happy . He likes to chase balls and sticks and birds . One day , Ben sees a new dog in the park . The new dog is big and black and has a short tail . He does not look friendly . He barks loudly at Ben and his friends . He tries to take their balls and sticks and birds . Ben is scared of the new dog . He does not want to play with him . He runs away and hides behind a tree . He frowns and tucks his tail between his legs . He is not cheerful anymore . Then , he hears a whistle . It is his owner , Sam . Sam is looking for Ben . He loves Ben and wants to play with him . He sees Ben behind the tree and calls his name . Ben hears Sam and runs to him . He is happy to see Sam . He wags his tail and licks Sam's face . He forgets about the new dog . He is cheerful again . Sam throws a ball for Ben . Ben chases the ball and brings it back to Sam . He plays with Sam and his friends . He does not frown anymore . He has fun . He is a cheerful dog .
Lily liked to soar in the sky . She had a big red kite that her dad gave her . She would run and run and let the kite fly high . She felt happy and free . One day , she went to the park with her dad and her kite . She saw a boy with a blue kite . He was also running and flying his kite . Lily wanted to play with him . She ran to him and said , " Hi , I like your kite . It is interesting . Can we be friends ? " The boy looked at Lily and her kite . He did not smile . He said , " No , I don't want to be friends . Your kite is boring . It is only red . My kite is better . It has stars and stripes . Go away . " Lily felt sad and angry . She did not like the boy . She said , " No , your kite is not better . My kite is faster and stronger . I will show you . " She ran and pulled her kite hard . She wanted to make it soar higher than the boy's kite . But she pulled too hard . Her kite broke . The string snapped and the kite fell to the ground . Lily cried . She lost her kite . She looked at the boy . He laughed . He said , " Ha ha , I told you . Your kite is boring and weak . My kite is the best . Bye bye , loser . " He ran away with his kite . Lily's dad came to her . He saw her kite on the ground . He saw her tears . He hugged her and said , " I'm sorry , Lily . Your kite is broken . Don't worry , we can get a new one . Come on , let's go home . " He picked up her kite and put it in his belt . He took her hand and walked to the car . Lily felt sad and sore . She did not want to soar anymore .
Sara and Tom like to jog with their dad . They put on their shoes and hats and go outside . Dad says , " Let's jog to the park and play on the swings . " They jog on the sidewalk and see many things . They see cars and dogs and flowers and birds . Sara and Tom point and say , " Look , dad , look ! " Dad smiles and says , " Yes , I see , they are very nice . " They get to the park and see many kids . Some kids are on the swings , some are on the slide , some are on the seesaw . Sara and Tom want to play too . They say , " Dad , can we play on the swings ? " Dad says , " Sure , but you have to wait your turn . There are other kids on the swings now . " Sara and Tom wait and watch the other kids . They see a girl with a pink dress and a boy with a blue shirt . They are swinging very high and laughing . Sara and Tom think they look fun . They say , " Hi , can we swing with you ? " The girl and the boy say , " Hi , yes , you can . We are almost done . Do you want to push us ? " Sara and Tom say , " Yes , we do . " They push the girl and the boy and make them go higher and higher . The girl and the boy say , " Wow , you are very good at pushing . Thank you ! " Sara and Tom say , " You are welcome ! " Then the girl and the boy stop swinging and get off . They say , " Now it's your turn . We will push you . " Sara and Tom say , " Thank you ! " They get on the swings and the girl and the boy push them . Sara and Tom feel the wind in their hair and the sun on their face . They swing very high and laugh . They say , " This is fun ! We like swinging ! " Dad watches them and smiles . He thinks , " They are very original . They make new friends easily . " He is proud of Sara and Tom . He says , " I'm glad you are having fun . But don't forget , we have to jog back home soon . " Sara and Tom say , " Okay , dad , we won't forget . But can we swing a little more ? " Dad says , " Okay , a little more . But then we have to go . " Sara and Tom say , " Okay , dad , we love you . " Dad says , " I love you too . "
Anna and her mom are going on a big plane to see grandma . Anna is very happy . She has a new backpack with a lot of colorful stickers . Mom has a big bag with clothes and books . " Anna , do you have your passport ? " mom asks . A passport is a small book with Anna's name and picture . It tells the people at the airport who she is and where she is going . " Yes , mom , I have it right here , " Anna says . She holds her passport in her hand . She likes to look at it . It has a blue cover and a shiny sticker . " Good girl . Now , let's go to the gate . We have to show our passports to the lady there . She will give us tickets for the plane , " mom says . She holds Anna's hand and they walk to the gate . " Hello , can I see your passports , please ? " the lady at the gate says . She has a red uniform and a big smile . " Here you go , " mom says . She gives the lady her passport and Anna's passport . " Thank you . You are going to grandma's country . How exciting ! Here are your tickets . Have a nice flight , " the lady says . She gives mom and Anna two colorful tickets with numbers and letters . " Thank you . Come on , Anna , let's go to the plane . It's time to fly , " mom says . " Yay ! I can't wait to see grandma . And the plane . And the clouds . And the birds . And the . . . " Anna says . She holds her passport and her ticket in her backpack . She is very happy . She and mom go to the plane . They are ready for their adventure .
Lily and Ben were playing with blocks . They liked to build towers and houses and cars . But they did not like to share . Lily wanted all the blue blocks and Ben wanted all the red blocks . They started to fight and pull the blocks from each other . " Give me that block ! " Lily shouted . " No , it's mine ! " Ben yelled . They were very grumpy and loud . Mom heard them and came to see what was wrong . She saw the mess of blocks and the angry faces of Lily and Ben . " What are you doing ? " Mom asked . " Why are you fighting ? " " Because she took my block ! " Ben said . " Because he took my block ! " Lily said . Mom shook her head . " That's not nice . You have to share and be kind . You have to attach the blocks together , not apart . Do you know what attach means ? " Lily and Ben looked at Mom . They did not know what attach meant . " It means to put things together , to make them stick . Like this . " Mom took two blocks and showed them how to attach them . " See ? Now they are one . And you can attach more blocks and make something bigger and better . Like a bridge or a train or a castle . But if you pull them apart , you break them and make them smaller and worse . Like a pile of rubbish . Do you understand ? " Lily and Ben nodded . They understood . They felt sorry for being grumpy and mean . They said sorry to each other and to Mom . They decided to attach the blocks together and share them . They made a big rainbow tower and a happy house and a fast car . They had fun and laughed and hugged . Mom smiled and praised them . " Good job , Lily and Ben . You learned how to attach and how to be friends . That's very important . I'm proud of you . Now , do you want a snack ? " Lily and Ben said yes and ran to the kitchen . They were hungry and thirsty . They thanked Mom for the snack and for the lesson . They learned that today was a good day to attach and to be happy .
Tim and Kim like to race . They race with their bikes , their feet , and their cars . They race in the park , in the street , and in the yard . They race every day , because racing is fun . One day , they see a big , white dog in the park . The dog is wild . It has no collar , no leash , and no owner . It runs fast and barks loud . It scares the other dogs and the people . Tim and Kim want to race with the dog . They get on their bikes and follow the dog . The dog sees them and runs faster . Tim and Kim pedal hard and try to catch up . They race past the trees , the swings , and the pond . They race with the wild , white dog . But the dog is too fast and too smart . It turns and runs into the woods . Tim and Kim cannot follow . They stop and look at each other . They are tired and out of breath . They smile and laugh . They had a good race with the wild , white dog . They go back to the park and race some more .
Lily and Tom are twins . They like to play with their toys and watch TV . But they do not like to eat their meal . Mom always makes them eat vegetables and soup . They think it is yucky and boring . One day , Mom is busy in the kitchen . She tells Lily and Tom to eat their meal and then watch TV . Lily and Tom do not listen . They sneak to the living room and turn on the TV . They watch a funny show with animals and laugh . Mom hears them and comes to the living room . She is angry . She turns off the TV and says , " Lily and Tom , you are very naughty . You did not eat your meal . You only watch TV . That is stupid . TV is not good for you . You need to eat your meal to grow big and strong . Now go back to the table and finish your meal . No more TV for you today . " Lily and Tom are sad and scared . They do not want to make Mom angry . They do not want to miss their show . They do not want to eat their meal . They cry and say , " But Mom , we do not like our meal . It is yucky and boring . We like TV . It is funny and fun . Please let us watch TV . Please do not be mad at us . " Mom shakes her head and says , " No , Lily and Tom . You have to eat your meal first . Then you can watch TV later . It is not stupid to eat your meal . It is smart . It makes you healthy and happy . TV is not everything . You can watch it another time . Now stop crying and go back to the table . I will help you eat your meal . And then I will hug you and tell you I love you . " Lily and Tom do not want to argue with Mom . They know she loves them and wants them to be good . They wipe their tears and go back to the table . They eat their meal with Mom's help . They do not like it , but they try to be brave . They hope Mom will let them watch TV tomorrow . They hope Mom will hug them and tell them she loves them . They love Mom too .
Anna and Ben are twins . They like to play in the park with their ball . One day , they see a big zigzag on the grass . It is a snake . The snake is sleeping in the sun . " Look , a snake ! " Ben says . He wants to touch it . He is not careful . " No , Ben , don't ! " Anna says . She is scared . She knows snakes can bite . She tries to stop him . But Ben does not listen . He runs to the snake . He disturbs it . The snake wakes up . It is angry . It hisses and coils . It shows its teeth . " Help , help ! " Ben cries . He is sorry . He runs back to Anna . Anna hugs him . She is glad he is safe . The snake slithers away . It wants to be alone . Anna and Ben learn a lesson . They should not disturb animals . They should be careful and respectful . They should listen to each other . They should play with their ball instead .
Sam likes to play with his red truck . He drives it on the floor and makes loud noises . He pretends he is a big truck driver . He has a lot of fun . One day , he sees a big hole in the yard . He thinks it is a good place to park his truck . He runs to the hole with his truck . He puts his truck in the hole and smiles . He thinks his truck is safe and happy . But then , he hears a loud sound . It is the water hose . His mom is watering the flowers . She does not see the hole or the truck . She sprays water on the hole . The hole starts to fill with water . Sam sees the water and gets scared . He runs to the hole and tries to get his truck . But the water is too deep and too fast . He cannot reach his truck . He feels his foot get wet . He cries . His mom hears him cry and turns off the hose . She sees the hole and the truck . She sees Sam's wet foot and sad face . She feels sorry for Sam . She says , " Oh , Sam , I'm sorry . I did not know your truck was there . It was an accident . Don't cry . We can get your truck out . It will be okay . " She goes to the shed and gets a big shovel . She digs the hole and makes the water go away . She finds the truck and gives it to Sam . She says , " Here is your truck , Sam . It is wet , but it is not broken . You can dry it and play with it again . It is a good truck . You are a good truck driver . " Sam takes his truck and hugs it . He says , " Thank you , mom . You are a good mom . You saved my truck . I love you . " He smiles . He learns a lesson . He learns that holes can fill with water . He learns that he should not park his truck in holes . He learns that his mom is serious about watering the flowers . He learns that his mom loves him and helps him . He is happy .
Tom and Lily like to play with their toys . They have many toys : cars , dolls , blocks , animals , and more . They make a lot of mess in their room . Their mom tells them to organize their toys . She says , " Put your toys in the right boxes . Make your room neat and tidy . Then you can go to the museum with me . " Tom and Lily do not listen to their mom . They think organizing is hard and boring . They want to go to the museum now . They say , " Mom , we are ready . Let's go to the museum . We want to see the dinosaurs and the paintings and the stars . " Their mom is not happy . She says , " No , you are not ready . Your room is still a mess . You have to organize your toys first . It is easy if you do it together . If you do not organize your toys , you cannot go to the museum . " Tom and Lily are angry . They do not want to organize their toys . They do not want to do it together . They start to fight . They throw their toys at each other . They break their toys . They make more mess . They make a lot of noise . Their mom hears the noise . She comes to their room . She sees the broken toys and the mess . She is very angry . She says , " That's it . You are not going to the museum . You are going to stay in your room and clean up . You are also going to say sorry to each other and to me . You have been very naughty and selfish . " Tom and Lily are sad . They cry . They wish they had listened to their mom . They wish they had organized their toys . They wish they had gone to the museum . They learn a lesson . They learn that organizing is easy and fun if they do it together . They learn that they have to respect their mom and their toys . They learn that they have to be good and kind to each other .
Lily loved to cook with her mom . They made healthy food like soup , salad and fruit . Lily liked to taste everything and help mom stir and chop . Mom taught Lily how to use the knife carefully and wash her hands . One day , Lily found mom's diary on the table . She was curious and opened it . She saw mom's writing and drawings . She wanted to read them , but the words were too hard . She tried to copy some of the words on a paper , but she made a mess . Mom came back and saw Lily with her diary and the paper . She was angry and sad . She said , " Lily , this is my diary . It is private . You should not touch it or write on it . You hurt my feelings . " Lily felt sorry and scared . She said , " Mom , I'm sorry . I just wanted to read your diary . I like your writing and drawings . I want to be like you . " Mom hugged Lily and said , " I love you , Lily . I know you want to learn , but you have to respect other people's things . If you want to read my diary , you can ask me . I can share some of it with you . And if you want to write and draw , you can use your own notebook . I can help you with the words . " Lily nodded and said , " Thank you , mom . I'm sorry again . Can we cook together again ? " Mom smiled and said , " Of course , we can . Cooking is fun and healthy . And it is something we can share . " The moral value of the story is : respect other people's privacy and belongings , and ask for permission before using them .
Anna and Ben like to help their mom cook . They have their own equipment : a small pot , a spoon , a bowl and a apron . Mom lets them cook some soup with carrots , potatoes and water . " Can I put some salt in the soup ? " Ben asks mom . " Yes , but only a little bit . Salt makes food tasty , but too much salt makes food bitter , " mom says . Anna wants to put some salt too . She takes the salt shaker and shakes it over the pot . But she shakes it too hard and a lot of salt falls into the soup . " Oh no , Anna , you put too much salt ! " Ben says . Anna feels sorry . She did not mean to put too much salt . She hopes the soup is still good . Mom tastes the soup . She makes a face . The soup is very bitter . " I'm sorry , mom . I put too much salt , " Anna says . " It's okay , Anna . It was an accident . We can fix the soup . We can add some more water and some milk . That will make the soup less bitter , " mom says . She adds some water and some milk to the pot and stirs the soup . She tastes it again . The soup is better now . It is not bitter anymore . " Good job , mom . The soup is yummy now , " Ben says . Anna smiles . She is happy that the soup is yummy . She and Ben help mom serve the soup in bowls . They eat the soup with bread and butter . They enjoy their lunch . They like to cook with mom .
Tom and Sue were having lunch with Mom . Mom gave them some salad and cheese . Tom liked cheese , but Sue did not . She made a face when she tasted it . " Yuck , this cheese is bitter ! " Sue said . " I don't want it . " Mom smiled and said , " You don't have to eat it , Sue . But you should try new things sometimes . You might like them . " Sue shook her head and pushed the cheese away . She picked up her fork and stabbed a tomato . She liked tomatoes . They were juicy and sweet . Tom saw Sue's fork and wanted to play with it . He reached for it and said , " Give me your fork , Sue . I want to make a tower with it . " Sue did not want to share her fork . She said , " No , Tom . This is my fork . You have your own fork . Go away . " Tom did not listen . He grabbed Sue's fork and pulled it hard . Sue held on to it and pulled it back . They both pulled and pulled until the fork snapped in half . " Ow ! " Tom cried . He dropped the fork and held his hand . He had cut his finger on the sharp edge of the fork . It hurt and it bled . Mom heard Tom's cry and came to see what happened . She saw the broken fork and Tom's finger . She said , " Oh , no ! What did you do , Tom ? You should not play with forks . They are sharp and dangerous . They are for eating , not for playing . " She took Tom to the sink and washed his finger . She put a band - aid on it and gave him a hug . She said , " I'm sorry , Tom . I hope you feel better soon . Next time , be careful with forks and ask nicely if you want to share something . " Tom nodded and said , " I'm sorry , Mom . I'm sorry , Sue . I won't play with forks again . Can I have some cheese , please ? " Sue looked at Tom and said , " You can have some of my cheese , Tom . But only if you say thank you . " Tom said , " Thank you , Sue . You are a good sister . " Sue smiled and said , " You are welcome , Tom . You are a good brother . " They both ate their cheese and salad and were happy . Mom was happy , too . She threw away the broken fork and got a new one for Sue . She said , " That's a good story , kids . Now , let's finish our lunch and have some cake for dessert . "
Anna and Ben are friends . They like to play with toys and books . One day , they see a poster on the wall . The poster says : " Come to the fair ! Win a prize ! Have fun ! " Anna and Ben want to go to the fair . They ask their moms to take them . Their moms say yes . They are happy . At the fair , they see many things . They see balloons , animals , games , and rides . They want to try everything . They play a game where they throw balls at cans . If they knock down all the cans , they win a prize . Anna throws a ball . She misses . She tries again . She misses again . She is sad . Ben throws a ball . He hits a can . He throws another ball . He hits another can . He throws the last ball . He knocks down all the cans . He wins a prize . He is happy . He looks at the prizes . He sees a teddy bear , a car , a doll , and a hat . He likes the teddy bear . It is brown and soft and cute . He picks the teddy bear . He goes to Anna . He says : " Look , Anna , I won a prize . It is a teddy bear . Do you like it ? " Anna says : " Yes , Ben , I like it . It is very cute . You are very good at the game . " Ben says : " Thank you , Anna . You are very nice . Do you want the teddy bear ? You can have it . It is for you . " Anna says : " Really , Ben ? For me ? Thank you , thank you , thank you ! You are very kind . I love the teddy bear . It is the best prize ever . " They hug each other . They hug the teddy bear . They are happy . They go to see more things at the fair . They have fun .
Lily and Max were playing outside with their ball . They liked to kick the ball to each other and catch it . The sky was blue and the sun was bright . Suddenly , they heard a loud boom . They looked up and saw dark clouds and flashes of lightning . They were scared and ran to their house . " Mama , mama , there is lightning ! " Lily cried . Mama came to the door and hugged them . " Don't worry , my loves , it's just a storm . It will pass soon . Come inside and we'll have some cookies and milk . " She took them to the kitchen and gave them a plate of cookies and two glasses of milk . She turned on the radio and played some calm music . She told them stories about the rain and the thunder and the lightning . Lily and Max listened and ate their cookies . They felt better and less scared . They learned that lightning was a big spark of electricity in the sky . They learned that thunder was the sound of lightning . They learned that rain was water falling from the clouds . They looked out the window and saw a rainbow . They smiled and hugged their mama . They thanked her for the cookies and the milk and the stories . They were happy and calm . The storm was over .
Tim and Lily were playing in the yard . They liked to jump in the big pile of leaves that dad made with the rake . The rake was long and had many teeth . It could make the leaves move and make a crunchy sound . But one day , when they were playing , they heard a loud roar . It came from behind the pile of leaves . Tim and Lily were scared . They thought it was a big , mean animal that wanted to eat them . They ran to the house and told mom . Mom said , " Don't worry , it's not a big , mean animal . It's just the neighbor's dog . He likes to play in the leaves too . He's very friendly and fluffy . Do you want to meet him ? " Tim and Lily nodded . They wanted to be brave and make a new friend . Mom took them to the pile of leaves and called the dog . The dog came out , wagging his tail and licking his nose . He was brown and white and had a red collar . " His name is Spot , " mom said . " He likes to play fetch and get belly rubs . Do you want to play with him ? " Tim and Lily smiled . They liked Spot . They took the rake and threw a stick for him . Spot ran after the stick and brought it back . He dropped it at their feet and rolled on his back . Tim and Lily gave him belly rubs and laughed . They were not scared anymore . They had found a new friend .
Lily liked to play with clay . She could make many things with clay . She could make balls , snakes , stars , and flowers . She liked to show her clay things to her mom and dad . They always said , " Good job , Lily ! " One day , Lily wanted to make a clay house . She had a big piece of clay . She rolled it and flattened it and cut it . She made four walls and a roof . She put them together with more clay . She made a door and a window . She was very happy with her clay house . She wanted to show her clay house to her mom and dad . She picked it up carefully and walked to the living room . But on the way , she tripped on a toy . She fell down and dropped her clay house . It broke into many pieces . Lily was very upset . She cried and cried . Her mom and dad heard her cry . They came to see what happened . They saw the broken clay house and the toy . They hugged Lily and said , " It's okay , Lily . We're sorry your clay house broke . We know you worked hard on it . But you can make another one . You can succeed again . We love you , Lily . " Lily stopped crying . She looked at her mom and dad . She felt better . She said , " Thank you , mom and dad . I love you , too . I will make another clay house . And I will be careful with the toy . " She smiled and hugged them back . She picked up the clay pieces and went back to her room . She was ready to try again .
Lily liked to stretch her lip with her fingers . She thought it was funny to make faces in the mirror . She also liked to stretch her lip when she was bored or angry . She did not know that stretching her lip too much could hurt it . One day , Lily went to the store with her mom . She saw a cheap toy that she wanted . It was a doll with long hair and a pink dress . She asked her mom to buy it for her , but her mom said no . Lily was very angry . She started to cry and scream . She also stretched her lip with her fingers . " Stop that , Lily , " her mom said . " You will hurt your lip if you stretch it too much . And you don't need that cheap toy . It will break soon . " Lily did not listen to her mom . She kept crying and stretching her lip . She wanted the toy so badly . She did not care about her lip . But then , something bad happened . Lily stretched her lip so much that it tore . She felt a sharp pain and saw blood on her fingers . She was scared and shocked . She stopped crying and looked at her mom . " Mommy , mommy , it hurts ! " she said . " My lip is broken ! " Her mom hugged her and took her to the bathroom . She cleaned her lip and put a band - aid on it . She also gave her a kiss and said she was sorry . " Lily , I'm sorry you hurt your lip , " her mom said . " But you have to be careful with your body . You can't stretch your lip like that . It's not good for you . " Lily nodded and said she was sorry too . She learned her lesson . She did not stretch her lip anymore . She also did not want the cheap toy anymore . She realized that it was not worth it . She was happy with her mom and her band - aid .
Lily and Tom were playing in the park . They saw a big tree with many colorful leaves . Lily wanted to climb the tree and see the sky . Tom was scared of the tree . He said , " No , Lily , do not climb the tree . It is too high and you can fall . " But Lily did not listen to Tom . She said , " I want to climb the tree . It is fun and pretty . You are a baby and you are afraid . " She ran to the tree and started to climb . She grabbed the branches and the leaves . She went higher and higher . Tom watched Lily from the ground . He was worried . He said , " Lily , come down . It is not safe . You can hurt yourself . " But Lily did not hear Tom . She was too busy climbing the tree . She was happy and proud . She said , " Look , Tom , I am on the top of the tree . I can see everything . I am the best climber . " But then , something bad happened . Lily saw a shiny thing on a branch . She reached for it . It was a needle . Someone had left it on the tree . Lily did not know what a needle was . She thought it was a toy . She touched the needle with her hand . It was sharp and it poked her . It made her bleed and it hurt a lot . Lily screamed and let go of the branch . She fell from the tree . She hit the ground hard . She was hurt and she cried . Tom ran to her and hugged her . He said , " Lily , I am sorry . You are hurt . I will get help . " He called for a grown - up . A grown - up came and took Lily to the doctor . The doctor gave her a band - aid and a hug . He said , " Lily , you are brave . But you should not climb the tree . It is dangerous . You should listen to your friend . He cares about you . " Lily was sad and sorry . She said , " I am sorry , Tom . You were right . I should not have climbed the tree . I was silly and mean . You are a good friend . Can you forgive me ? " Tom said , " I forgive you , Lily . You are my friend . But please , do not climb the tree again . It is not worth it . " Lily said , " I promise , Tom . I will not climb the tree again . I will play with you on the ground . It is safer and more fun . " They hugged and smiled . But Lily's hand still hurt and she had a scar . She learned a hard lesson . Climbing the tree was a bad idea .
Lily and Ben were playing with their toys in the living room . They liked to make up stories with their dolls and cars . Lily had a big box of photos that her mom gave her . She liked to look at them and see the faces of her family and friends . " Look , Ben , this is a photo of me and you when we were babies , " Lily said , holding up a picture . " We were so small and cute . Do you remember ? " Ben shook his head . He did not remember being a baby . He only remembered being a big boy who could run and jump and talk . " Mom says we were very lively babies . We liked to laugh and play and make noise . We still do , right ? " Lily asked . Ben nodded . He liked to laugh and play and make noise with Lily . They were best friends . " Who is this ? " Ben asked , pointing to another photo . It showed a man with a big smile and a hat . He was holding Lily in his arms . " That's my dad . He lives far away now . He works on a big boat . He sends me letters and stickers sometimes . I miss him a lot , " Lily said , feeling sad . Ben hugged Lily . He knew how it felt to miss someone . His dad also lived far away . He worked on a big plane . He also sent him letters and stickers sometimes . " Maybe our dads will come back soon . Maybe they will play with us and take us to the park . That would be fun , right ? " Ben said , trying to cheer Lily up . Lily smiled . She hoped Ben was right . She loved her dad and wanted to see him again . " Maybe they will . And maybe they will take a photo of us together . Then we can put it in the box and look at it when we miss them . That would remind us of them , right ? " Lily said . Ben agreed . He liked that idea . He liked photos . They reminded him of happy times . He and Lily decided to make a card for their dads . They used crayons and stickers and wrote " We love you " on it . They put the card in an envelope and asked their mom to mail it . They hoped their dads would like it . They hoped their dads would come back soon . They hoped they would be a happy family again .
Lily liked to touch things . She liked to touch soft things , like her teddy bear and her blanket . She liked to touch hard things , like her blocks and her spoon . She liked to touch warm things , like her mom and her soup . She liked to touch cold things , like her ice cream and her snowman . But Lily did not like to touch sour things . Sour things made her face scrunch up and her tongue sting . She did not like lemons , or vinegar , or pickles . She did not like sour things at all . One Sunday , Lily went to the park with her dad . She saw a big tree with yellow fruits . She wondered what they were . She asked her dad , and he said they were grapefruits . He said they were good for you , but they were a little sour . Lily wanted to try one . She asked her dad to pick one for her . He did , and he peeled it for her . He gave her a slice and said , " Be careful , it might be sour . " Lily took a bite . She felt a burst of juice in her mouth . She felt a sour taste on her tongue . She felt her face scrunch up and her tongue sting . She did not like it . She spit it out and said , " Yuck ! Sour ! No like ! " Her dad laughed and hugged her . He said , " It's okay , Lily . You don't have to like everything . Here , have some water . It will make your tongue feel better . " Lily drank some water . She felt her tongue feel better . She smiled and said , " Thank you , dad . Water good . Grapefruit bad . " Her dad smiled and said , " You're welcome , Lily . I love you . Let's go play on the swings . " Lily nodded and said , " Yes , dad . I love you too . Swings fun . " They went to the swings and had a good time . Lily forgot about the sour grapefruit . She only remembered the sweet water and the warm hug . She was happy . It was a good Sunday .
Sam and Mia love to play at the beach . They like to make sand castles , find shells and splash in the water . One day , they see a big surf board near the shore . It is red and shiny and has a rope on it . " Wow , look at that ! " Sam says . " Can we try it ? " " I don't know , " Mia says . " It looks hard to use . And maybe it belongs to someone . " " Let's ask Mom , " Sam says . They run to their mom , who is sitting on a towel with a book . " Mom , can we use that surf board ? Please ? " Mom looks at the surf board and smiles . " Sure , you can use it . But be careful , okay ? And stay close to me . The surf board is for the water , not for the sand . " " Yay , thank you , Mom ! " Sam and Mia say . They grab the rope and drag the surf board to the water . They take turns to sit on it and paddle with their hands . They feel the waves lift them up and down . They laugh and shout with joy . " Look , Mom , I'm surfing ! " Sam says . " Me too , me too ! " Mia says . They have so much fun that they don't notice a hidden rock under the water . When a big wave comes , the surf board hits the rock and flips over . Sam and Mia fall into the water . They are scared and wet . They cry for help . " Mom , Mom , help us ! " they scream . Mom hears them and runs to the water . She grabs the rope and pulls the surf board back . She helps Sam and Mia to get on it . She hugs them and calms them down . " Are you okay , my sweeties ? " she asks . " Yes , Mom , we're okay , " Sam and Mia say . " But we're sorry . We broke the surf board . " Mom looks at the surf board and sees a crack on it . She sighs and says , " Don't worry , we can fix it . Or we can buy a new one . The important thing is that you are safe and happy . But next time , be more careful , okay ? And watch out for hidden rocks . " " Okay , Mom , we will , " Sam and Mia say . They hug Mom and say , " We love you , Mom . " " I love you too , my darlings , " Mom says . She smiles and says , " Now , let's go and have some ice cream . How does that sound ? " " Yummy , yummy , ice cream ! " Sam and Mia say . They release the rope and leave the surf board on the shore . They follow Mom to the ice cream truck . They forget about the surf board and the hidden rock . They only think about the ice cream and the fun they had . They are happy .
Lily and Ben are best friends . They like to play pretend . Sometimes they pretend to be animals , or pirates , or superheroes . Today they pretend to be doctors . Lily has a white coat and a stethoscope . Ben has a bandage and a hat . They find a teddy bear and put it on the couch . " This is our patient , " Lily says . " He is sick . We have to make him better . " Ben nods . He looks at the teddy bear with his stethoscope . " He has a fever , " he says . " And a sore throat . And a cough . " He wraps the bandage around the teddy bear's neck . " There , that will help . " Lily smiles . She takes her finger and pretends it is a needle . " He also needs a shot , " she says . " It will make him strong and healthy . " She gently pokes the teddy bear with her finger . " Don't worry , it won't hurt . " The teddy bear does not say anything . He just looks at Lily and Ben with his big eyes . He thinks they are very kind . He feels better already . He gives them a hug and a kiss . " Thank you , doctors , " he says . " You are the best . " Lily and Ben hug him back . They are happy . They like to play pretend . They like to help their friends . They are kind doctors .
Tom and Lily were at the beach with their mom and dad . They liked to play in the sand and look at the water . Sometimes they saw fish or birds or boats . But today they saw something big and gray and spouty . It was a whale ! " Wow , look at that whale ! " Tom said . " It is so big and strong and loud ! " " I want to see it closer ! " Lily said . " Maybe it will talk to us or play with us ! " Mom and dad shook their heads . " No , Lily , you can't go near the whale . It is too far and too deep and too dangerous . The whale might not like it if you bother it . It might get angry or scared or hurt you . " " But why ? " Lily asked . " The whale looks nice and friendly . It is just swimming and splashing and having fun . Maybe it is lonely and wants some friends . " Tom decided to agree with Lily . He thought mom and dad were ignorant . They did not know anything about whales . He wanted to learn more about them and make them happy . He said to Lily , " Let's go find the whale . We can sneak away when mom and dad are not looking . We can swim to it and say hello and give it a hug . " Lily nodded . She thought Tom was brave and smart . She wanted to go with him and see the whale . She said to Tom , " OK , let's go . But we have to be quick and quiet . We don't want mom and dad to stop us . " They waited until mom and dad were busy with their books and towels . Then they ran to the water and jumped in . They swam as fast as they could , following the whale's spout . They did not see the big waves or the sharp rocks or the other animals . They only saw the whale . They got closer and closer to the whale . They saw its eyes and mouth and fins and tail . They saw its skin and teeth and blowhole . They thought it was beautiful and amazing . They smiled and waved and shouted , " Hi , whale ! We are Tom and Lily ! We want to be your friends ! " The whale heard them and saw them . It did not smile or wave or shout back . It did not want to be their friends . It did not like them at all . It thought they were annoying and rude and stupid . It decided to teach them a lesson . It opened its mouth and sucked them in . It swallowed them whole . It felt them squirm and scream in its belly . It felt satisfied and proud and happy . It spouted a big jet of water and dived deeper into the sea . It left behind mom and dad , who were looking for Tom and Lily . They did not see the whale or hear their children . They only saw the water and felt the fear and the sadness .
Lila and Max were best friends . They liked to play in the park with their toys . One day , they saw a new girl with a big doll . The doll had long hair , a pretty dress and shiny shoes . Lila and Max looked at the doll and felt envious . " Wow , look at her doll ! " Lila said . " It is so big and nice . I wish I had a doll like that . " " Me too , " Max said . " Maybe we can ask her to play with us and share her doll . " They walked over to the new girl and said hello . The new girl smiled and said her name was Mia . She held her doll close to her waist . " Hi , Mia . We are Lila and Max . Can we play with you and your doll ? " Lila asked . " Sure , you can play with me . But you can't touch my doll . She is mine and I love her very much . She is the best doll in the world , " Mia said . Lila and Max felt sad . They wanted to play with the doll , but Mia did not want to share . They thought Mia was not very nice . They looked at each other and had an idea . " Okay , Mia . We will play with you . But we have a game for you . It is called 'seem' . Do you know how to play ? " Max asked . " No , I don't . How do you play ? " Mia asked . " It is easy . You just have to say what things seem like . For example , I can say 'the sky seems blue' or 'the grass seems green' . Do you understand ? " Max explained . " I think so . It sounds fun . Let's play , " Mia said . " Okay , you go first . What does your doll seem like ? " Lila asked . Mia looked at her doll and smiled . She said , " My doll seems like a princess . She has beautiful hair , a lovely dress and sparkly shoes . She is the most wonderful doll ever . " Lila and Max nodded . They waited for Mia to look away . Then they quickly grabbed the doll and ran away . They laughed and said , " Your doll seems like ours now . Bye , Mia ! " Mia was shocked and angry . She shouted and cried . She wanted her doll back . She did not like the game of 'seem' . She thought Lila and Max were very mean . She wished she had shared her doll with them .
Anna loved animals . She wanted to be a veterinarian when she grew up . She liked to read books about animals and watch them on TV . She also liked to play with her stuffed animals and pretend they were sick or hurt . She would use her toy stethoscope and bandages to make them feel better . One day , Anna went to the park with her mom and dad . She saw a lot of animals there . She saw birds , squirrels , dogs , and cats . She wanted to pet them all , but her mom and dad told her to be careful and ask the owners first . Anna was impatient . She wanted to touch the animals and hear their hearts . She saw a big dog with a red collar . He looked friendly and wagged his tail . Anna ran towards him , but he barked and growled . He did not like strangers . Anna was scared and cried . The dog's owner came and apologized . He said his dog was nervous and needed to see a veterinarian . He said the veterinarian would help his dog feel calm and happy . Anna felt sorry for the dog . She wondered what a veterinarian was . She asked her mom and dad . They explained that a veterinarian was a doctor for animals . They said a veterinarian could check the animals' health and give them medicine or surgery if they needed it . They said a veterinarian could also teach the owners how to take care of their animals and prevent them from getting sick or hurt . Anna was amazed . She wanted to learn more about veterinarians . She decided to be more patient and respectful with the animals . She thought that maybe one day , she could be a veterinarian too . She smiled and hugged her stuffed animals . She said she would take them to the veterinarian if they ever felt bad . She said she loved animals and wanted to help them .
Ben and Lily were playing in the park . They liked to swing on the swings and slide on the slide . But today , one of the swings was broken . The seat had a big hole in it and the chain was loose . " Oh no , we can't swing on this one , " said Ben . " It's not safe . " " Maybe we can repair it , " said Lily . " We can find some things to fix it . " They looked around the park and found some sticks , some leaves , and some string . They tried to patch the hole with the leaves and tie the chain with the string . They worked hard and had fun . " Look , we did it ! " said Lily . " We repaired the swing ! " They ran to the swing and tried to sit on it . But as soon as they did , the leaves fell off and the string snapped . The swing was still broken . " Oops , it didn't work , " said Ben . " We need something stronger . " Just then , a man came to the park . He had a toolbox and a ladder . He was wearing a hat that said " Park Ranger " . " Hello , kids , " he said . " I'm here to repair the swing . I have the right tools and materials . Do you want to help me ? " " Yes , please ! " said Ben and Lily . " We tried to repair it , but we couldn't . " " That's very kind of you to try , " said the man . " But sometimes you need a professional . Here , you can hold this wrench and this hammer . And you can hand me these nails and this board . Together , we can make the swing good as new . " Ben and Lily helped the man repair the swing . They learned how to use the tools and how to measure and cut the board . They were very careful and listened to the man's instructions . " There , we're done , " said the man . " Now the swing has a new seat and a strong chain . Do you want to try it ? " " Yes , thank you ! " said Ben and Lily . " You're very kind to repair the swing for us and let us help you . " They climbed on the swing and swung high and low . They felt the wind in their hair and the sun on their faces . They laughed and smiled . They were happy to play on the repaired swing .
Once upon a time , there was a little boy named Tim . Tim was ready to go to the park with his mom . He put on his jacket and shoes , and they walked to the park together . At the park , Tim saw a big tree . He wanted to climb it . But a girl named Sally was there too . She said , " I want to climb the tree first ! " Tim and Sally both wanted to climb the tree at the same time . They did not know what to do . Tim's mom had an idea . She said , " I have two tickets for the ice cream truck . Whoever climbs the tree first gets a ticket ! " Tim and Sally raced to the top of the tree . It was very cold up there , and they started to shiver . But they both wanted the ticket , so they kept going . In the end , Tim and Sally both reached the top at the same time . They were both so cold from shivering that they decided to share the tickets . They climbed down the tree and got their ice cream . They ate their ice cream together and became friends . From that day on , they always played together at the park .

Once upon a time , there was a unique cat named Bob . Bob was not like other cats . He liked to help his friends get better when they were sick . Bob was a patient cat who wanted to improve the lives of his friends . One day , Bob's friend , a little bird named Sue , was not feeling well . Sue said , " Bob , I don't feel good . Can you help me improve ? " Bob thought for a moment and said , " Yes , Sue . I will be patient and help you feel better . " Bob and Sue tried many things to help Sue feel better . They tried resting , eating yummy food , and drinking water . But nothing seemed to work . Sue was still not feeling well . Bob started to feel sad because he could not help his friend . But then , he had an idea . " Sue , let's try singing a happy song . Maybe that will help you feel better . " Sue and Bob sang the happy song together . Slowly , Sue started to feel better . The happy song had worked ! Bob was so happy that he could help his friend improve . From that day on , Bob and Sue knew that their unique friendship could help them face any problem .
Once upon a time , there was a little monkey named Bobo . Bobo loved to climb trees and dance in the jungle . One day , Bobo saw a big tree and wanted to climb it . As he started to climb , he met a foolish bird named Chirpy . " Hey , Bobo ! " said Chirpy . " Why are you climbing this tree ? " " I want to dance at the top ! " Bobo replied . Chirpy laughed and said , " You can't dance up there ! It's too high ! You will fall ! " Bobo was sad but didn't want to give up . He thought of a plan . He asked Chirpy , " Can you help me ? We can dance together ! " Chirpy thought about it and agreed to help Bobo . They climbed the tree together and reached the top . They danced and had so much fun . In the end , Bobo and Chirpy became best friends , and they learned that with teamwork , they could do anything .

" The cat nodded and jumped onto the armchair . Lily and the cat sat on the green armchair , and they read the book together . They laughed and had so much fun . From that day on , Lily and the cat were the best of friends , and they always shared the green armchair .

The dog saw the cat playing with the cardboard and wanted it too . The dog and the cat began to fight over the cardboard box . The cat had an idea . The cat tried to reverse the box to hide inside . But the dog was too strong . The dog took the cardboard box away from the cat . The cat was very sad and had no more cardboard to play with . The end .
Once upon a time , there was a big white octopus . He lived in the deep blue sea . He had many friends who loved to play with him . One day , the white octopus met a little fish who was very sad . The white octopus asked the little fish , " Why are you sad , little fish ? " The little fish said , " I am sad because I have no friends to play with . " The white octopus felt bad for the little fish . He said , " Don't worry , little fish . I will be your friend , and we can play together ! " So , the white octopus and the little fish played together all day long . They had so much fun . The little fish was not sad anymore . They became best friends forever . The moral of the story is : always be kind and help others , and you will make many friends .

Once upon a time , in a small garden , there lived a lazy insect named Izzy . Izzy did not like to work hard like the other insects . She loved to sit in the sun and watch the flowers grow . One day , Izzy found a big , red apple . She was so happy and wanted to eat it all by herself . But , she was too lazy to carry the apple to her home . So , she left it under the sun to eat it later . The next day , Izzy came back to eat her apple . But , the hot sun had spoiled the apple . It was not good to eat anymore . Izzy was sad and hungry . She learned that being lazy can spoil good things . From that day on , Izzy worked hard with the other insects and always shared her food .

The small dog became their friend too . And they all played together , having lots of fun .

At the park , she saw a shiny penny on the ground . " Wow ! " she said , " A penny ! " Lucy picked up the penny and stared at it . It was so shiny and pretty . She showed it to her mom . " Look , Mom ! I found a penny ! " Her mom smiled and said , " That's great , Lucy ! You can save it in your piggy bank . " While Lucy was playing , a little boy named Tim came up to her . He saw the penny in her hand and said , " Hey , that's my penny ! I lost it earlier ! " Lucy didn't want to give it back , but she knew it was the right thing to do . " Okay , here you go , " she said , handing the penny to Tim . Tim thanked Lucy and they became friends . They played together at the park all day long . And even though Lucy didn't have the shiny penny anymore , she was happy because she made a new friend .

So , she thought of an idea to make her day more exciting . Lucy decided to order the sun to do something different . She went outside and shouted , " Sun , please make today more fun and not boring ! " To her surprise , the sun heard her and started to change colors . It turned red , then blue , then green , and then yellow . It was like a big , bright rainbow in the sky . Lucy was so happy that the sun listened to her . She danced and played under the colorful sun all day long . Her day was not boring anymore . It was the most fun day she ever had . And from that day on , whenever Lucy felt bored , she would remember the day the sun changed colors just for her .

She felt sad for the bug and didn't know what to do . Lily thought for a moment and then carefully picked up the bug . She went outside and found a nice spot in the grass to put the bug to rest . Lily learned that it is important to be kind to all living things , even the smallest ones like bugs . She also learned that sometimes , we need to let go of things we love . From that day on , Lily always remembered to be kind and gentle to everyone and everything around her .

She felt very sad and asked her mommy for help . " Mommy , I can't find Ann . Can you help me look for her ? " Her mommy said , " Of course , Amy . Let's look together . " They looked in the kitchen , the living room , and even in the bathroom , but they could not find Ann . Then , Amy's mommy had an idea . " Let's look in the attic , " she said . They climbed up the stairs to the attic and looked around . There , in the corner , they found Ann ! Amy was so happy to have her doll back . She hugged her mommy and said , " Thank you , Mommy ! " From that day on , Amy always made sure to keep her toys safe and sound . She played with Ann and Spot all the time , and they all lived happily ever after .

Once upon a time , there was a little duck named Ducky . Ducky lived near a small pond . One day , Ducky went to the pond to swim , but the pond was empty . Ducky was sad , so he decided to find out where the water went . Ducky met a frog named Froggy . Ducky asked Froggy , " Do you know where the water went ? " Froggy said , " I don't know , but let's find it together ! " So , Ducky and Froggy decided to look for the water . They walked and walked until they found a big rock . Behind the rock , they saw a hole in the ground . Ducky and Froggy decided to push the rock to cover the hole . When the hole was covered , the rain came , and the pond filled with water again . Ducky and Froggy were very happy . They jumped into the pond and swam together . They became best friends and played in the pond every day . And they lived happily ever after .

" So Lily washed her hair and then used the comb to make her hair look nice and pretty . After combing her hair , Lily saw that her hair was still wet . She felt proud and happy that she could get ready all by herself . Her mom and dad were proud of her too , and they all had a great day together .

The bird fell down and could not fly anymore . The cop saw the man and the bird . The cop was sad because the bird could not fly . The man was bad and the yellow bird was hurt . The cop took the man away , but the bird did not have a happy ending .
One day , a lazy cat named Tom saw a rail . He thought it was a fun place to play . He jumped on the rail and walked on it . Tom was very happy . A bird named Sally saw Tom on the rail . She flew down and said , " Hi Tom ! Do you want to hear a joke ? " Tom said , " Yes , I like jokes ! " Sally told a funny joke and they both laughed . Later , Tom saw a big dog coming near the rail . He remembered Sally's joke and decided to play a trick on the dog . Tom jumped off the rail and hid . When the dog came closer , Tom jumped out and yelled , " Boo ! " The dog was scared and ran away . Tom and Sally laughed and played on the rail for the rest of the day .
Once upon a time , in a small place , there was an ugly bug . The bug had a big nose and funny legs . It lived under a rock where it could hide from the sun . The ugly bug liked to poke things with its nose . One day , the ugly bug met a little bird . The bird was sad because it lost its friend . The ugly bug wanted to help the bird . So , they went to look for the bird's friend together . They walked and walked until they found the bird's friend . The bird's friend was stuck in a hole . The ugly bug used its nose to poke the dirt and make the hole bigger . The bird's friend was free ! The birds were happy and thanked the ugly bug . From that day on , they all lived together in the small place and became the best of friends .
Once upon a time , there was a little boy named Tim . Tim had a flashlight . He loved his flashlight very much . He used it when it was dark . One day , Tim's mom told him they were going to visit Grandma's house . Tim was very happy . He knew it would be dark soon , so he took his flashlight with him . When Tim and his mom got to Grandma's house , they saw that the lights were off . It was dark inside . Tim's mom asked him to help her find the way . Tim was excited . He knew his flashlight would be useful . He took it out and turned it on . Tim's flashlight made a bright light . It helped them see in the dark . They found Grandma in the living room . She was happy to see them . Tim felt proud that he could help his mom and Grandma . They all had a great time together . And Tim knew his flashlight was always there to help when it was dark .
Once upon a time , there was a strong boy named Tim . He loved to play outside with his friends all day . One day , Tim was playing with his ball when it went into his friend's yard . His friend , Sarah , was not home , but Tim went into her yard to get the ball anyway . As night came , Tim went home and forgot to tell Sarah about the ball . The next day , Sarah saw the ball in her yard and knew that Tim had been there . She was sad that Tim did not ask her first . Tim saw that Sarah was sad and said , " I am sorry , Sarah . I should have asked you first . Can you forgive me ? " Sarah smiled and said , " Yes , Tim . I forgive you . Let's play together now . " So Tim and Sarah played happily , and they were careful to ask each other before going into each other's yards .
Once upon a time , in a small village , there lived a little boy named Tim . He loved to play with his dog , Spot . They played all day in the sun . One day , Tim and Spot found a big box of cream . Tim was very happy . Tim wanted to keep the cream all for himself . He thought of a plan . He took the cream and went to the woods . Tim dug a big hole and buried the cream . But , Spot saw him . Spot looked troubled . He knew it was not good to hide things from others . The next day , Tim went back to get the cream . But , he could not find it . He was sad . Spot came and showed Tim where the cream was . They went back to the village and shared the cream with their friends . Everyone was happy . Tim learned that sharing is better than hiding things from others . This made Tim feel good inside .

They both went to his house and started to clean . They cleaned the floor , the windows , and the walls . When they were done , the house looked very nice . Tom and Lucy became good friends . They played and cleaned together every day . And they lived happily ever after .

Tom's mom called out , " Tom , be careful with the dog ! " But Tom was not scared . He threw the ball and the dog ran to get it . They played together and had lots of fun . The tree would shake its leaves , and Zoom would try to dry them with his wind . " Zoom , can you dry my leaves today ? " the tree asked . " Yes , I can ! " said Zoom . So , Zoom went very fast around the tree , making a lot of wind . The leaves became dry , and the tree was happy . After playing , Zoom was hungry . He saw a delicious apple on the ground . Zoom picked up the apple with his little door and ate it . " Yum , this is delicious ! " said Zoom . The tree smiled , happy to see his friend enjoy the apple . And they played together until the sun went down .

Tom started eating the melon very fast . Tim paused and looked at Tom . He thought of a plan . Tim said , " Tom , look ! A big bird is taking your toy ! " Tom stopped eating and looked away . While Tom was looking for the bird , Tim ate the melon very fast . When Tom looked back , the melon was gone . Tim smiled and said , " I won ! The bird was not real . I tricked you ! " Tom was sad but learned that he should not always believe what others say .

Mia was very happy to see the clown . The clown said , " Hi Mia ! Do you want to play a game ? " Mia nodded her head and said , " Yes , please ! " The clown showed her a soft ball and asked her to throw it at a pile of soft toys . Mia threw the ball and won a toy . The clown then reminded Mia to always be kind to her friends . Mia smiled and said , " Thank you , Mr . Clown ! I will be kind to everyone . " The clown was happy to hear this and waved goodbye as Mia left the tent with her new toy .
Once upon a time , there was a big billboard in the middle of the town . The billboard used to display many colorful pictures . One day , a new picture was put up on the billboard . This picture was not nice . It was disgusting . The people in the town did not like it . A little boy named Tim saw the billboard on his way to the park . He said , " Yuck ! That picture is not good . " Tim's friend , Sally , agreed . " Yes , it is not nice . We should do something . " So , Tim and Sally went to the town's leader and told him about the disgusting picture on the billboard . The leader listened to Tim and Sally . He said , " You are right . We should not display bad pictures in our town . Let's change it . " So , the leader asked a painter to make a new , beautiful picture for the billboard . The people in the town were happy to see the new picture . The moral of the story is to always speak up when you see something wrong , so that it can be fixed .
Once upon a time , in a small yard , there was a chubby dog named Spot . Spot liked to run and play near the fence . One day , he saw a big red ball on the other side of the fence . Spot really wanted that ball . Spot said to his friend , the little bird , " I want that ball , but I can't get it . " The little bird said , " I can help you . I will fly over the fence and push the ball to you . " Spot was very happy and said , " Thank you , little bird ! " The little bird flew over the fence and pushed the ball . But , the ball was too big and heavy . The little bird tried very hard , but the ball did not move . Suddenly , a surprise came . A big cat jumped out from behind the ball and caught the little bird . Spot was sad , and he knew he could not help his friend . The little bird was gone , and the big cat took her away . Spot learned that sometimes , surprises can be bad and not all things we want are good for us .
Once upon a time , there was a fat cat named Tom . Tom loved to play with bubbles . One sunny day , Tom went outside to play with his friends . Tom's friend , a little girl named Lily , had a big bottle of bubble soap . She blew a big bubble into the air . " Wow , look at that big bubble ! " said Lily . Tom jumped and tried to catch the bubble with his paw . As the fat cat jumped , he tried to slap the bubble . " Pop ! " went the bubble , and Tom fell to the ground . " Oops ! " said Tom , laughing . " I got it ! " Lily laughed too and blew more bubbles for Tom to slap and pop . They played with the bubbles all day , having lots of fun . Tom would slap and pop the bubbles , and Lily would laugh and blow more . As the sun began to set , they knew it was time to go home . " Bye , Tom ! " said Lily . " Bye , Lily ! " said Tom . And they both went home , happy after a fun day of playing with bubbles .

" Tim asked . His mom said , " Not now , Tim . We need to get food first . Maybe later . " They went around the store , putting food in the cart . Tim could not stop thinking about the hidden toy . He knew he had to be good , so his mom would let him grab it . At the end of the shopping , Tim's mom said , " You have been good , Tim . Now you can grab the toy . " Tim was so happy ! Once upon a time , in a big pond , there was a lazy frog named Lily . She liked to sit on her big green leaf and watch the fish swim by . One sunny day , a little bug flew by and asked , " Why are you so lazy , Lily ? " Lily just shrugged and said , " I like being lazy . It's fun to watch the fish and enjoy the sun . " The little bug didn't understand and flew away . The next day , a big rain came . All the other frogs were busy swimming and playing in the water . But Lily just sat on her leaf , being lazy . A small fish swam up to her and said , " Come play with us , Lily ! " Lily shrugged again and said , " No , thank you . I like being lazy on my leaf . " The small fish swam away , sad that Lily didn't want to play . In the end , Lily stayed lazy on her leaf , watching the world go by . She liked her simple , lazy life , and that was okay .

She said , " I will lend you my scary mask . Then you can scare people . " The fake wolf was happy . Lily lent the fake wolf her scary mask . The fake wolf put on the mask and scared people . The fake wolf was not sad anymore . Lily and the fake wolf became good friends .

Once upon a time , in a small house , there lived a kind mom and her little boy named Tim . They loved to bake together . One day , they decided to make a yummy pastry . While they were making the pastry , a wild bird flew into their kitchen . " Oh no ! " said Tim . " What do we do , Mom ? " The wild bird was scared and flew all around the room . Mom thought for a moment and then said , " Let's wrap the pastry and take it outside . Maybe the wild bird will follow us . " So , they carefully wrapped the pastry in a cloth . Tim and his mom slowly walked outside with the wrapped pastry . The wild bird saw them and flew out the door . They all went to the backyard and unwrapped the pastry . The wild bird chirped happily . " Thank you , Mom ! " said Tim . They shared the pastry with the wild bird , and it became their new friend . From that day on , they all enjoyed many more baking adventures together .

" Tim jumped into the puddle with a big splash . The water went everywhere . Tim laughed and jumped again . Tim's mom saw him playing in the puddle . She came outside and said , " Tim , it's time to come inside and be tidy . " Tim said , " Okay , Mom . " He went inside , took off his boots , and got cleaned up . Once upon a time , in a big forest , lived a small bunny named Ben . Ben was a very polite bunny . He loved to eat berries and play with his friends . One day , Ben found a big red berry under a bush . He was so happy ! But then , his friend , a squirrel named Sam , came by . Sam was very hungry and asked Ben , " Can I have some of your berry , please ? " Ben thought for a moment and then said , " Yes , you can have half of my berry . " Ben and Sam sat down and shared the berry . They were both very happy . After they ate , they decided to lie down under a tree and rest . As they lay there , they talked and laughed together . Soon , other animals in the forest heard about Ben's kind act . They all wanted to be friends with Ben and Sam . The forest became a much happier place because everyone learned to share and be polite , just like Ben . The moral of the story is to always be polite and share with others . It can make the world a happier place for everyone .

He used his fingers to help him count . Tim said , " One , two , three , four , five , six . Wow , the bug has six legs ! " Tim's mom heard him and came outside . She saw the bug too . " Yes , Tim , that's a bug called an ant , " she said . Tim looked at his mom and smiled . Tim and his mom watched the ant walk away . They saw more ants and counted their legs too . They had fun counting together . Soon , the gray clouds went away and the sun came out . Tim and his mom enjoyed the rest of the day playing outside .

Once upon a time , in a small town , there was a little girl named Amy . Amy had a big file of drawings . She loved to draw pictures of animals , trees , and houses . One day , she wanted to draw something new and exciting . Amy asked her mom , " Mom , what should I draw today ? " Her mom thought for a moment and said , " I recommend you draw a dangerous animal , like a lion or a bear . But remember , you should never go near them in real life . " So , Amy took out her file and started to draw a big , dangerous lion . She drew its sharp teeth , big paws , and long tail . When she was done , she showed her drawing to her mom . Her mom was very happy and said , " Good job , Amy ! You drew a very dangerous lion , but you are safe because it's just a drawing . " Amy felt proud of her drawing and put it in her file with the other pictures . She learned that it was fun to draw dangerous things , but she should always be careful and listen to her mom's recommendations . From that day on , Amy continued to draw many more pictures , always asking her mom for recommendations . They had lots of fun together , and Amy's file of drawings grew bigger and bigger .

The wind blew hard , and the rain was so heavy that the leaf could not talk to his friends . The little leaf felt sad and lonely . After the rain stopped , the sun came out and the forest was happy again . The little leaf could talk to his friends again . They all laughed and played together . The little leaf was no longer sad , and the heavy rain was just a memory .

" Lucy agreed and said , " Yes , let's save the bird ! " Fluffy climbed the tree and carefully picked up the bird in her mouth . She brought the bird down to Lucy , who gently took it from Fluffy . The bird was so happy to be free . It chirped a thank you to Fluffy and Lucy , and then flew away . Fluffy and Lucy were proud of their rescue . They knew they had done a nice thing for the bird . Once upon a time , there was a kind , old cop named Tom . He lived in a small town with many friends . One day , Tom was walking down the street when he saw a little girl named Lily . She looked sad and lost . Tom went up to Lily and asked , " Why are you sad , little girl ? " Lily said , " I can't choose which toy to buy . I only have enough money for one . " Tom thought for a moment and said , " I can help you choose the best toy . " Tom and Lily went to the toy store . There were many toys to choose from . There were dolls , cars , and even a big teddy bear ! Tom said , " Why don't you choose the teddy bear ? It's soft and cuddly . " Lily smiled and said , " Yes , I like the teddy bear ! The parents bought Sarah a dictionary . Sarah was very happy and looked through it . The words in the dictionary were all very clear and she started to learn them . One day , Sarah wanted to show off her new words to her friends . She took the dictionary and went to school . When Sarah arrived to school , she noticed her friends were playing with a ball . She asked to join in . Sarah didn't know that her friends were using some of the big words she had learnt . But when the teacher heard Sarah's words , he shouted at her and told Sarah off . Sarah was very upset . She ran away and never returned to school . The dictionary was never seen again .

" Mom said in a gentle , encouraging voice . " It's harmless . You can do it ! " Molly stepped closer to the edge of the pool , but then she stopped . She was scared .
