The moral of the story is that even though we can be curious and ignorant , we should always listen to the wise advice of others . Ignoring it may have negative consequences .

Once upon a time , there was a girl named Alice and her dog , Spot . They were best friends and played all the time . One day , they were on a walk by the river when Alice noticed a threatening noise coming from the woods . She stopped in her tracks and looked up at Spot , who was sitting by her feet , panting heavily . Frightened , she called out for help , but no one was around . Suddenly , the noise got louder and Alice was really scared . She started to cry and held Spot closer to her . Then , a bright light shine through the trees and out came a giant . At first , it looked like it was going to be a bad thing , but then it spoke . " Don't be scared , little one . " it said in a gentle voice . " It is just me . I was out for a stroll and heard you calling . " Alice relaxed and wiped away her tears . The giant smiled and said goodbye , then disappeared back into the woods . Alice and Spot continued on with their walk . From then on , whenever they heard a strange noise , Alice smiled and said , " It's just the friendly giant . " Spot wagged his tail in agreement and they both were no longer scared .

Once upon a time there lived a large rabbit called Wilson . He lived in a sunny meadow near a large bucket . The moral of the story is to not regret what has already happened , but to learn from it and appreciate the good things you already have .

Once upon a time , there lived a young girl named Lucy . She was an adventurous spirit , with a very large urge to explore . Every day , she found something new and exciting to do . One day , she was folding her laundry when she noticed an interesting looking blanket in the corner . She decided to unfold it , and when she did , out flew a spicy fragrance . Immediately , Lucy was urged to investigate . She stepped through the blanket and found herself in a foreign land . " What is this place ? " she said to herself . She saw exotic plants , fairy lights , and the warmest of welcomes . Suddenly , a voice called out . " Welcome to SpiceLand ! I'm so glad you came to visit , " the voice said . Lucy realized that it was the Queen of SpiceLand , inviting her to explore and have fun . She happily accepted the invitation and spent the whole day learning about the spicy world . When it was time to go home , Lucy knew she had to leave , as much as she didn't want to . But she was determined to come back soon and fold the blanket , eager to come and explore SpiceLand again .
One day , Tommy and Mummy were playing in the garden . Suddenly , rain clouds filled the sky and it started to rain . Mummy said , " Tommy , let's go inside so you don't get soaked ! " Tommy was sad , he said , " But Mummy I wanted to keep playing ! Can I stay ? " Mummy felt bad , she was being a bit harsh . But she said , " No , you'll get too wet . Come on , grab your buckle and we'll go in . " Tommy reluctantly grabbed his buckle and they ran inside . As they stepped over the threshold they heard a loud cheer . It was Papa and he had been watching them from the window . He said , " That was great ! Now come inside , both of you , so you don't get soaked ! " Tommy and Mummy laughed , they were so happy . They soon were snuggled up on the sofa with a blanket , enjoying a cozy cup of hot chocolate . They were so happy that everyone stayed away from the rain .
Once upon a time , there was a small panda who did not feel like he belonged anywhere . " Where should I go ? " he asked . He looked around , but he couldn't find anyone to help him . Just then , an elephant spotted the panda and said â€œCome with me ! â€ The elephant and the panda walked down the path until they came to a big tree . " This is your home now , " the elephant said . The panda thanked the elephant and went inside the tree . Inside the tree , the panda discovered a wonderful place . He saw a pond , a jungle , even a garden . He felt so happy that he finally found a place that he belonged . The elephant waved goodbye and told the panda , " I will come and check on you from time to time ! " The small panda stayed in the tree and found friends to play with . Every day , he felt happier and more like he belonged .
Once upon a time , there was a little girl who was very anxious . She lived in a nice house with her family , but what she wanted more than anything was a pet . One day , she heard a sound coming from outside . It was a soft meow . She ran outside to see what it was , and there was a small pink kitten ! The girl was so excited , she wanted to take it home . She asked her mom if she could keep it and her mom said that she could , if the kitten wanted to come . The girl started to gently pet the kitten and it soon purred and snuggled up against her . This was a sign to the girl that the kitten wanted to come with her . She brought the pet inside and named it Pink . From then on , the two were best friends and were never apart . Pink brought a lot of joy to the little girl and her family .
Once upon a time , there was a bear called Bob . Bob lived in a peaceful building near the woods . When Bob was not out exploring , he loved to read books . One day , Bob was reading his favorite book , when he suddenly heard a noise outside . Bob quickly ran to the window and saw a fox and a rabbit . The two animals were fighting and were making a lot of noise . Bob hurried downstairs and shouted , " Stop fighting ! " But the animals seemed not to hear him . Bob tried again and again , but still the animals kept on fighting . Suddenly , a little squirrel popped out of the tree near the building . It was peeking up at Bob and seemed to be telling him something . Bob listened carefully and suddenly understood what the squirrel was saying : " Read to them ! " Bob smiled and picked up his book . He walked up to the animals and began reading . As his gentle voice filled the air around them , the fox and the rabbit soon stopped fighting . They sat down and listened while Bob read them the story . When Bob finished , the animals thanked him , hopped away , and never fought again . Bob smiled and returned to his peaceful building , happy that he could help .
Once there were two friends , Sally and Jimmy . Sally wanted to give Jimmy a present . She found a nice box , but it was too heavy for her . So , she decided to go to the store to weigh the present . At the store , the shopkeeper had a machine that weighed things . Sally put the present on the machine and it weighed 5 pounds . The shopkeeper was very organized and he helped Sally put the present in a bag . The two friends were very happy when Sally gave Jimmy the present . Jimmy said it was the best gift he ever had . From then on , Sally and Jimmy always weighed presents before giving them .

" Ellie and her dad were exploring a swamp . It was quiet and still . They could hear birds chirping in the distance . Suddenly , Ellie saw a movement in the murky water . " Dad , look ! " she shouted excitedly . He peered into the water to see a creature stirring in the swamp . His face furrowed in worry and he told Ellie to stay still as he tried to move closer . Suddenly , out of the water appeared a huge crocodile . Its sharp teeth glistened in the sunshine . Ellie gasped in surprise and before she knew what was happening , the croc had snapped up her dad in its powerful jaws . Ellie started to scream , but the swamp was silent . In a matter of seconds the croc had swallowed her dad whole , never to be seen again . That day , Ellie learnt to always stay still when exploring the swamp .
Once upon a time there was an ancient tire sitting in the middle of a hill . It was very old , and was covered in mud . One day , a little frog happened to come by and saw the tire . He asked the tire why it was so sad and dirty , and the tire replied that it had been sitting here for so long , and no one had remembered to bathe it . The little frog thought carefully and spoke . " It's never too late to have a bath , " he said . " Even if you are ancient and forgotten , you can still be cleaned and restored to new life ! " So with that , the frog called on his friends , and they all helped to bathe the tire . After they were done , all of them marveled at the sight . Instead of being a forgotten and mud - covered tire , the old tire was now clean and sparkling ! The tire thanked the little frog and his friends for giving it such a special gift , and reminded them that sometimes even the ancient can be renewed and brought back to life .

Tim was suffering . He had been all alone for days , so he went to the store to buy a magazine . He was so happy to see the colorful pages full of stories that he didn't realize how late it had become . The sun was beginning to set , and the streets started to become quiet . But Tim didn't have time to worry about the fading light . He was too busy looking through the magazine . Suddenly , a sharp noise startled Tim . He looked up to see a tall figure behind him . It was a robber , and he was not happy to see Tim . The robber grabbed the magazine and ran away . Tim was left alone in the quiet street with nothing . He had been enjoying his magazine , but now all he had was the suffering of being robbed . He wished he had left the store earlier , but now it was too late .
Once there was a little girl named Sue . Sue was three years old and she was very happy . She had a box full of toys that she would play with all day long . One day , Sue wanted to play with her box but she couldn't find it . She looked all over her room but the box was nowhere to be found . Sue asked her mom , " Where is my box ? " Her mom replied , " I gave it away . I thought it was stupid . " Sue was very sad . She burst into tears and said , " Mom , I never wanted you to give away my box ! " But it was too late . Her mom couldn't get it back . And Sue was left without her box . She never found it again .
John was a very smart little boy . One day , he asked his dad for something to drink . His dad said he could have some coffee . John thanked his dad , and then sipped the coffee . It was not sweet and it was dry . John thought the dry coffee tasted funny , so he asked his dad for sugar to make it sweeter . Dad gave him some sugar to put in the coffee , and after stirring it in , the coffee tasted much better . John thanked his dad again , and then he enjoyed his sweetened coffee . Dad smiled at John and said he was very happy to make the coffee just the way John liked it . John was very pleased , and he thanked his Dad once more . He finished his coffee , hugged his dad and went off to play .
